describe("Inventeringsomgång — rapportens slutlagerregel", () => {
  test("årsinventering använder start minus avrapporterat plus skannat utanför start", async () => {
    const omrade = await skapaOmrade("TEST_RapportArs");
    const fackA = await skapaFack(omrade.id, "A1");
    const fackB = await skapaFack(omrade.id, "B1");
    const found = await skapaPost({ omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fackA.id, pktNr: "P-R-FOUND", antal: 2, m3: 0.2 });
    const misplaced = await skapaPost({ omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fackA.id, pktNr: "P-R-MISPLACED", antal: 3, m3: 0.3 });
    await skapaPost({ omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fackA.id, pktNr: "P-R-BEHALL", antal: 4, m3: 0.4 });
    await skapaPost({ omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fackA.id, pktNr: "P-R-UNDECIDED", antal: 5, m3: 0.5 });
    const avrapporterad = await skapaPost({ omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fackA.id, pktNr: "P-R-AVRAPP", antal: 6, m3: 0.6 });
    const conflict = await skapaPost({ omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fackA.id, pktNr: "P-R-CONFLICT", antal: 7, m3: 0.7 });

    const omgangId = await skapaArsinventering("Rapport årsregel", omrade.id);
    const sessionA = await startaSession(omrade.id, fackA.id, omgangId);
    const sessionB = await startaSession(omrade.id, fackB.id, omgangId);

    await skanna(sessionA, "P-R-FOUND");
    await db.update(lagerPosterTable).set({ fackId: fackB.id }).where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, misplaced.id));
    await skanna(sessionB, "P-R-MISPLACED");
    await skanna(sessionA, "P-R-CONFLICT");
    await db.update(lagerPosterTable).set({ fackId: fackB.id }).where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, conflict.id));
    await skanna(sessionB, "P-R-CONFLICT");

    // Created after the global start snapshot: it is included in the final
    // stock, but must retain the annual "not in startsnapshot" status.
    const [afterStart] = await db
      .insert(lagerPosterTable)
      .values({ pktNr: "P-R-AFTER-START", traslag: "Furu", antal: 8, m3: "0.8", omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fackB.id })
      .returning();
    posterIds.push(afterStart.id);
    await skanna(sessionB, "P-R-AFTER-START");

    const behall = await api("POST", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/saknade/behall`, {
      body: { pktNrLista: ["P-R-BEHALL"] },
    });
    expect(behall.status).toBe(200);
    await api("POST", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/dubbelskanningar/P-R-CONFLICT/los`, {
      body: { valdSessionId: sessionB },
    });
    await avslutaSession(sessionA);
    await avslutaSession(sessionB);
    expect((await api("POST", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/avsluta`)).status).toBe(200);

    const avrapportera = await api("POST", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/saknade/avrapportera`, {
      body: { pktNrLista: [avrapporterad.pktNr] },
    });
    expect(avrapportera.status).toBe(200);

    type Rapport = {
      totals: { unikaPaket: number; saknadePaketUtanBeslut: number; varning: string | null };
      slutlager: { pktNr: string; status: string; placering: { fackId: number | null }; summering: { styck: number } }[];
      paket: { pktNr: string; status: string; iSlutlager: boolean; placering: { fackId: number | null }; summering: { styck: number } }[];
    };
    const fore = await api<Rapport>("GET", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/rapport`);
    expect(fore.status).toBe(200);
    expect(fore.data.totals.saknadePaketUtanBeslut).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(1);
    expect(fore.data.totals.varning).toContain("saknade paket saknar beslut");
    const scenarioPktNr = new Set([
      "P-R-FOUND",
      "P-R-MISPLACED",
      "P-R-BEHALL",
      "P-R-UNDECIDED",
      "P-R-CONFLICT",
      "P-R-AFTER-START",
    ]);
    expect(fore.data.slutlager.filter((p) => scenarioPktNr.has(p.pktNr))).toHaveLength(6);
    expect(fore.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-R-AVRAPP")).toMatchObject({
      status: "Avrapporterat",
      iSlutlager: false,
    });
    expect(fore.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-R-BEHALL")?.status).toBe("Saknas – Behåll i lager");
    expect(fore.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-R-UNDECIDED")?.status).toBe("Saknas – inget beslut");
    expect(fore.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-R-AFTER-START")?.status).toBe("Skannat – ej i startsnapshot");
    expect(fore.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-R-CONFLICT")?.placering.fackId).toBe(fackB.id);

    // A post-close change in live lager_poster must not alter frozen report
    // quantities or placement.
    await db.update(lagerPosterTable).set({ antal: 999, m3: "99", fackId: fackB.id }).where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, found.id));
    const efter = await api<Rapport>("GET", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/rapport`);
    expect(efter.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-R-FOUND")?.summering).toEqual(
      fore.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-R-FOUND")?.summering,
    );
  });

  test("kontrollrapporten använder endast fryst sessionsscope och beslut", async () => {
    const omrade = await skapaOmrade("TEST_RapportKontroll");
    const utanförOmrade = await skapaOmrade("TEST_RapportKontrollUtanför");
    const fack = await skapaFack(omrade.id, "A1");
    const utanförFack = await skapaFack(utanförOmrade.id, "U1");
    await skapaPost({ omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fack.id, pktNr: "P-K-R-BEHALL", antal: 2, m3: 0.2 });
    const avrapporterad = await skapaPost({ omradeId: omrade.id, fackId: fack.id, pktNr: "P-K-R-AVRAPP", antal: 3, m3: 0.3 });
    await skapaPost({ omradeId: utanförOmrade.id, fackId: utanförFack.id, pktNr: "P-K-R-UTANFOR", antal: 4, m3: 0.4 });

    const omgangId = await skapaOmgang("Kontroll rapport", omrade.id);
    const sessionId = await startaSession(omrade.id, fack.id, omgangId);
    expect((await api("POST", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/saknade/behall`, {
      body: { pktNrLista: ["P-K-R-BEHALL"] },
    })).status).toBe(200);
    await avslutaSession(sessionId);
    expect((await api("POST", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/avsluta`)).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await api("POST", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/saknade/avrapportera`, {
      body: { pktNrLista: [avrapporterad.pktNr] },
    })).status).toBe(200);

    const report = await api<{
      totals: { unikaPaket: number };
      paket: { pktNr: string; status: string; iSlutlager: boolean }[];
      slutlager: { pktNr: string }[];
    }>("GET", `/inventeringsomgangar/${omgangId}/rapport`);
    expect(report.status).toBe(200);
    expect(report.data.totals.unikaPaket).toBe(1);
    expect(report.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-K-R-BEHALL")).toMatchObject({
      status: "Saknas – Behåll i lager",
      iSlutlager: true,
    });
    expect(report.data.paket.find((p) => p.pktNr === "P-K-R-AVRAPP")).toMatchObject({
      status: "Avrapporterat",
      iSlutlager: false,
    });
    expect(report.data.paket.map((p) => p.pktNr)).not.toContain("P-K-R-UTANFOR");
    expect(report.data.slutlager.map((p) => p.pktNr)).toEqual(["P-K-R-BEHALL"]);
  });
});


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/__tests__/lagerManualCreate.test.ts ================

import type { Server } from "node:http";
import express from "express";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test, vi } from "vitest";
import { eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  db,
  lagerHistorikTable,
  lagerPosterTable,
  pool,
} from "@workspace/db";

vi.mock("../../app", () => ({
  requireAdmin: (
    _req: express.Request,
    _res: express.Response,
    next: express.NextFunction,
  ) => next(),
}));

import lagerRouter from "../lager";

type ApiResult<T> = { status: number; data: T };

const suffix = `${Date.now()}-${process.pid}`;
const createdIds: number[] = [];
let server: Server;
let baseUrl: string;

function payload(pktNr: string, overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {}) {
  return {
    pktNr,
    datumIn: "2026-09-15",
    produktion: "MANUELL",
    profil: "Regel",
    traslag: "Gran",
    kvalitet: "C24",
    utforande: "4RH",
    nomTjocklek: 45,
    nomBredd: 95,
    actTjocklek: 44,
    actBredd: 94,
    antal: 100,
    langdDm: 48,
    ...overrides,
  };
}

async function request<T>(
  path: string,
  body: Record<string, unknown>,
  method = "POST",
): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const text = await response.text();
  const data = (text ? JSON.parse(text) : null) as T;
  if (response.status === 201) {
    if (Array.isArray(data)) {
      createdIds.push(...data.map((row) => (row as { id: number }).id));
    } else {
      createdIds.push((data as { id: number }).id);
    }
  }
  return { status: response.status, data };
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json());
  app.use((req, _res, next) => {
    (req as unknown as { userRole: string; userId: string }).userRole = "admin";
    (req as unknown as { userRole: string; userId: string }).userId = "test-user";
    next();
  });
  app.use(lagerRouter);
  app.use((_error: unknown, _req: express.Request, res: express.Response, _next: express.NextFunction) => {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Avsiktligt testfel" });
  });
  server = app.listen(0);
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.once("listening", resolve));
  const address = server.address();
  if (!address || typeof address === "string") {
    throw new Error("Testservern startade inte");
  }
  baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`;
});

afterAll(async () => {
  if (server) {
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) =>
      server.close((error) => (error ? reject(error) : resolve())),
    );
  }
  if (createdIds.length > 0) {
    await db.delete(lagerHistorikTable).where(inArray(lagerHistorikTable.lagerId, createdIds));
    await db.delete(lagerPosterTable).where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.id, createdIds));
  }
  await pool.end();
});

describe("manuell lagerregistrering med paketnummerspärr", () => {
  test("batchuppdatering nekar skyddade, okända, blandade och tomma patchar utan skrivning", async () => {
    const created = await request<{ id: number }>(
      "/",
      payload(`TEST-BATCH-SAKERHET-${suffix}`, { plats: "Ursprunglig plats" }),
    );
    const invalidPatches = [
      { raderadAt: new Date().toISOString() },
      { m3Pris: 1 },
      { pktNr: "KAPAT" },
      { datumUt: "2026-09-16", plats: "Blandad" },
      { okandKolumn: true },
      {},
    ];

    for (const patch of invalidPatches) {
      const result = await request<{ error: string }>(
        "/batch",
        { ids: [created.data.id], patch },
        "PUT",
      );
      expect(result.status).toBe(400);
    }

    const [unchanged] = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, created.data.id));
    expect(unchanged.plats).toBe("Ursprunglig plats");
    expect(unchanged.raderadAt).toBeNull();
    expect(unchanged.datumUt).toBeNull();
    expect(unchanged.pktNr).toBe(`TEST-BATCH-SAKERHET-${suffix}`);
  });

  test("batchuppdatering behåller giltig platsflytt och utleverans", async () => {
    const created = await request<{ id: number }>(
      "/",
      payload(`TEST-BATCH-GILTIG-${suffix}`, { plats: "Före flytt" }),
    );

    expect((await request("/batch", { ids: [created.data.id], patch: { plats: "Efter flytt" } }, "PUT")).status)
      .toBe(200);
    expect((await request("/batch", { ids: [created.data.id], patch: { datumUt: "2026-09-16" } }, "PUT")).status)
      .toBe(200);

    const [updated] = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, created.data.id));
    expect(updated.plats).toBe("Efter flytt");
    expect(updated.omradeId).toBeNull();
    expect(updated.fackId).toBeNull();
    expect(updated.datumUt).toBe("2026-09-16");
  });

  test("ett nytt paketnummer kan skapas", async () => {
    const result = await request<{ id: number; pktNr: string }>(
      "/",
      payload(`TEST-MANUELL-NY-${suffix}`),
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(201);
    expect(result.data.pktNr).toBe(`TEST-MANUELL-NY-${suffix}`);
  });

  test("en andra fysisk post med samma aktiva paketnummer får 409", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-DUBBEL-${suffix}`;
    expect((await request("/", payload(pktNr))).status).toBe(201);
    const duplicate = await request<{ error: string }>("/", payload(pktNr, { antal: 101 }));
    expect(duplicate.status).toBe(409);
    expect(duplicate.data.error).toBe(`Paketnummer ${pktNr} finns redan i lagret.`);
  });

  test("två samtidiga anrop kan inte skapa två fysiska paket", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-RACE-${suffix}`;
    const results = await Promise.all([
      request("/", payload(pktNr)),
      request("/", payload(pktNr, { antal: 102 })),
    ]);
    expect(results.map((result) => result.status).sort()).toEqual([201, 409]);

    const rows = await db
      .select({ id: lagerPosterTable.id })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNr));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  test("skiftlägesvarianter använder samma lås och kan inte skapas samtidigt", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-CASE-${suffix}`;
    const results = await Promise.all([
      request("/", payload(pktNr.toUpperCase())),
      request("/", payload(pktNr.toLowerCase(), { antal: 103 })),
    ]);
    expect(results.map((result) => result.status).sort()).toEqual([201, 409]);
  });

  test("ett verkligt paket med flera längder skapas atomiskt i samma batch", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-FLERLANGD-${suffix}`;
    const result = await request<Array<{ id: number; pktNr: string; langdDm: number }>>(
      "/manual-batch",
      {
        poster: [
          payload(pktNr, { antal: 100, langdDm: 42 }),
          payload(pktNr, { antal: 80, langdDm: 48 }),
        ],
      },
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(201);
    expect(result.data).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(result.data.map((row) => row.langdDm)).toEqual([42, 48]);
    expect((await request("/", payload(pktNr))).status).toBe(409);
  });

  test("ett paketnummer som bara finns i papperskorgen får återanvändas", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-PAPPERSKORG-${suffix}`;
    const [deleted] = await db
      .insert(lagerPosterTable)
      .values({
        ...payload(pktNr),
        nomTjocklek: "45",
        nomBredd: "95",
        actTjocklek: "44",
        actBredd: "94",
        langdDm: "48",
        raderadAt: new Date(),
      })
      .returning();
    createdIds.push(deleted.id);

    const result = await request<{ id: number }>("/", payload(pktNr));
    expect(result.status).toBe(201);
  });

  test("redigering av en befintlig post blockeras inte av dess eget paketnummer", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-REDIGERA-${suffix}`;
    const created = await request<{ id: number }>("/", payload(pktNr));
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);

    const updated = await request<{ id: number; pktNr: string; utforande: string }>(
      `/${created.data.id}`,
      { pktNr, utforande: "Planhyvlat" },
      "PUT",
    );
    expect(updated.status).toBe(200);
    expect(updated.data.pktNr).toBe(pktNr);
    expect(updated.data.utforande).toBe("Planhyvlat");
  });

  test("redigering till ett annat aktivt paketnummer får 409", async () => {
    const first = await request<{ id: number }>(
      "/",
      payload(`TEST-MANUELL-REDIGERA-FRAN-${suffix}`),
    );
    const occupiedPktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-REDIGERA-TILL-${suffix}`;
    expect((await request("/", payload(occupiedPktNr))).status).toBe(201);

    const result = await request<{ error: string }>(
      `/${first.data.id}`,
      { pktNr: occupiedPktNr.toLowerCase() },
      "PUT",
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(409);
    expect(result.data.error).toContain("finns redan");
  });

  test("återställning får 409 om paketnumret har återanvänts", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-ATERSTALL-${suffix}`;
    const [deleted] = await db
      .insert(lagerPosterTable)
      .values({
        ...payload(pktNr),
        nomTjocklek: "45",
        nomBredd: "95",
        actTjocklek: "44",
        actBredd: "94",
        langdDm: "48",
        raderadAt: new Date(),
      })
      .returning();
    createdIds.push(deleted.id);
    expect((await request("/", payload(pktNr.toLowerCase()))).status).toBe(201);

    const result = await request<{ error: string }>(
      `/${deleted.id}/aterstall`,
      {},
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(409);
    expect(result.data.error).toContain("finns redan");
  });

  test("alla längdrader i samma borttagningsgrupp återställs atomiskt", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-ATERSTALL-GRUPP-${suffix}`;
    const deletedAt = new Date();
    const deletedRows = await db
      .insert(lagerPosterTable)
      .values([
        {
          ...payload(pktNr, { langdDm: 42 }),
          nomTjocklek: "45",
          nomBredd: "95",
          actTjocklek: "44",
          actBredd: "94",
          langdDm: "42",
          raderadAt: deletedAt,
        },
        {
          ...payload(pktNr, { langdDm: 48 }),
          nomTjocklek: "45",
          nomBredd: "95",
          actTjocklek: "44",
          actBredd: "94",
          langdDm: "48",
          raderadAt: deletedAt,
        },
      ])
      .returning();
    createdIds.push(...deletedRows.map((row) => row.id));

    expect((await request(`/${deletedRows[0].id}/aterstall`, {})).status).toBe(200);
    const active = await db
      .select({ id: lagerPosterTable.id })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNr));
    expect(active.filter((row) => deletedRows.some((deleted) => deleted.id === row.id))).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  test("en separat historisk manuell dubblett kan inte återaktiveras", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-HISTORISK-DUBBEL-${suffix}`;
    const active = await request<{ id: number }>("/", payload(pktNr, { langdDm: 42 }));
    const [historicalDuplicate] = await db
      .insert(lagerPosterTable)
      .values({
        ...payload(pktNr, { langdDm: 48 }),
        nomTjocklek: "45",
        nomBredd: "95",
        actTjocklek: "44",
        actBredd: "94",
        langdDm: "48",
        raderadAt: new Date(),
      })
      .returning();
    createdIds.push(historicalDuplicate.id);

    expect(active.status).toBe(201);
    expect((await request(`/${historicalDuplicate.id}/aterstall`, {})).status).toBe(409);
  });

  test("borttagning av en historisk manuell felrad lämnar Paketlapp-paketet aktivt", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-RADERA-KOLLISION-${suffix}`;
    const paketlappRows = await db
      .insert(lagerPosterTable)
      .values([
        {
          ...payload(pktNr, { langdDm: 42 }),
          nomTjocklek: "45",
          nomBredd: "95",
          actTjocklek: "44",
          actBredd: "94",
          langdDm: "42",
          produktion: "PAKETLAPP_HYVLERI",
          externSourceId: `TEST-SOURCE-${suffix}`,
          externRevision: 1,
        },
        {
          ...payload(pktNr, { langdDm: 48 }),
          nomTjocklek: "45",
          nomBredd: "95",
          actTjocklek: "44",
          actBredd: "94",
          langdDm: "48",
          produktion: "PAKETLAPP_HYVLERI",
          externSourceId: `TEST-SOURCE-${suffix}`,
          externRevision: 1,
        },
      ])
      .returning();
    const [manualDuplicate] = await db
      .insert(lagerPosterTable)
      .values({
        ...payload(pktNr, { antal: 308 }),
        nomTjocklek: "45",
        nomBredd: "95",
        actTjocklek: "44",
        actBredd: "94",
        langdDm: "48",
      })
      .returning();
    createdIds.push(...paketlappRows.map((row) => row.id), manualDuplicate.id);

    expect((await request(`/${manualDuplicate.id}`, {}, "DELETE")).status).toBe(204);
    const rows = await db
      .select({ id: lagerPosterTable.id, raderadAt: lagerPosterTable.raderadAt })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNr));
    expect(rows.find((row) => row.id === manualDuplicate.id)?.raderadAt).not.toBeNull();
    expect(
      rows
        .filter((row) => paketlappRows.some((paketlapp) => paketlapp.id === row.id))
        .every((row) => row.raderadAt === null),
    ).toBe(true);
  });

  test("en borttagen längdrad från en atomisk manuell batch kan återställas", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-MANUELL-ATERSTALL-BATCH-${suffix}`;
    const created = await request<Array<{ id: number }>>("/manual-batch", {
      poster: [
        payload(pktNr, { langdDm: 42 }),
        payload(pktNr, { langdDm: 48 }),
      ],
    });
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);

    const deletedId = created.data[0].id;
    expect((await request(`/${deletedId}`, {}, "DELETE")).status).toBe(204);
    expect((await request(`/${deletedId}/aterstall`, {})).status).toBe(200);

    const restored = await db
      .select({ id: lagerPosterTable.id, raderadAt: lagerPosterTable.raderadAt })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNr));
    expect(restored).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(restored.every((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toBe(true);
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/__tests__/paketlapp.test.ts ================

import "../../app";

import { randomUUID } from "node:crypto";
import type { Server } from "node:http";
import express from "express";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import { eq, inArray, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  db,
  externPaketIdempotencyTable,
  lagerPosterTable,
  pool,
} from "@workspace/db";
import { ensurePaketlappFields } from "../../migrations/paketlapp";
import externRouter from "../extern";
import lagerRouter from "../lager";

type ApiResult<T> = { status: number; data: T };
type PaketlappResponse = {
  sourceId: string;
  clientRequestId: string;
  revision: number;
  operation: "upsert" | "delete";
  result: "created" | "updated" | "deleted";
  pktNr: string;
  rows: Array<{
    id: number;
    pktNr: string;
    behandling: string | null;
    utforande: string | null;
    langdDm: number | null;
    antal: number | null;
  }>;
};

const suffix = `${Date.now()}-${process.pid}`;
const testApiKey = `paketlapp-test-${suffix}`;
const originalApiKey = process.env.EXTERN_API_KEY;
const sourceIds: string[] = [];
const idempotencyKeys: string[] = [];
const directPostIds: number[] = [];
let server: Server;
let baseUrl: string;

function createPayload(
  overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {},
): Record<string, unknown> {
  const sourceId = randomUUID();
  sourceIds.push(sourceId);
  return {
    sourceId,
    clientRequestId: randomUUID(),
    revision: 1,
    operation: "upsert",
    pktNr: `TEST-PAKET-${suffix}-${randomUUID()}`,
    datumIn: "2026-09-02",
    produktion: "PAKETLAPP_HYVLERI",
    profil: "Tegelläkt",
    traslag: "Gran",
    kvalitet: null,
    behandling: "Buntat",
    nomTjocklek: 25,
    nomBredd: 50,
    actTjocklek: 25,
    actBredd: 48,
    plats: null,
    anteckning: null,
    langder: [
      { antal: 440, langdDm: 48, m3: 2.64, actM3: 2.5344, lpm: 2112 },
    ],
    ...overrides,
  };
}

function createDeletePayload(
  sourceId: string,
  pktNr: string,
  overrides: Record<string, unknown> = {},
): Record<string, unknown> {
  return {
    sourceId,
    clientRequestId: randomUUID(),
    revision: 2,
    operation: "delete",
    pktNr,
    produktion: "PAKETLAPP_HYVLERI",
    ...overrides,
  };
}

async function postPaket<T = PaketlappResponse>(
  body: Record<string, unknown>,
  idempotencyKey = randomUUID(),
): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
  idempotencyKeys.push(idempotencyKey);
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/extern/paket`, {
    method: "POST",
    headers: {
      "content-type": "application/json",
      "x-api-key": testApiKey,
      "idempotency-key": idempotencyKey,
    },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const text = await response.text();
  return {
    status: response.status,
    data: (text ? JSON.parse(text) : null) as T,
  };
}

async function requestLager<T = Record<string, unknown>>(
  method: string,
  path: string,
  body?: Record<string, unknown>,
): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const text = await response.text();
  return {
    status: response.status,
    data: (text ? JSON.parse(text) : null) as T,
  };
}

async function requestExternLager<T = Record<string, unknown>>(
  method: "GET" | "POST",
  path: string,
  body?: Record<string, unknown>,
): Promise<ApiResult<T>> {
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/extern/lager${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: {
      "content-type": "application/json",
      "x-api-key": testApiKey,
    },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const text = await response.text();
  return {
    status: response.status,
    data: (text ? JSON.parse(text) : null) as T,
  };
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  process.env.EXTERN_API_KEY = testApiKey;
  await ensurePaketlappFields();
  await ensurePaketlappFields();

  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json());
  app.use(externRouter);
  app.use(lagerRouter);
  app.use((_error: unknown, _req: express.Request, res: express.Response, _next: express.NextFunction) => {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Avsiktligt testfel" });
  });
  server = app.listen(0);
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.once("listening", resolve));
  const address = server.address();
  if (!address || typeof address === "string") {
    throw new Error("Testservern startade inte");
  }
  baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`;
});

afterAll(async () => {
  if (server) {
    await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) =>
      server.close((error) => (error ? reject(error) : resolve())),
    );
  }
  if (idempotencyKeys.length > 0) {
    await db
      .delete(externPaketIdempotencyTable)
      .where(inArray(externPaketIdempotencyTable.idempotencyKey, idempotencyKeys));
  }
  if (sourceIds.length > 0) {
    await db
      .delete(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, sourceIds));
  }
  if (directPostIds.length > 0) {
    await db
      .delete(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.id, directPostIds));
  }
  if (originalApiKey === undefined) delete process.env.EXTERN_API_KEY;
  else process.env.EXTERN_API_KEY = originalApiKey;
  await pool.end();
});

describe("Paketlapp extern paketendpoint", () => {
  test("den additiva migrationen ändrar inte befintliga lagervärden", async () => {
    const [before] = await db
      .insert(lagerPosterTable)
      .values({
        pktNr: `TEST-MIG-${suffix}`,
        profil: "Plank",
        traslag: "Furu",
        antal: 10,
        langdDm: "42",
      })
      .returning();
    directPostIds.push(before.id);

    await ensurePaketlappFields();
    await ensurePaketlappFields();

    const [after] = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, before.id));
    expect(after).toMatchObject({
      pktNr: before.pktNr,
      profil: before.profil,
      traslag: before.traslag,
      antal: before.antal,
      langdDm: before.langdDm,
      behandling: null,
      externSourceId: null,
      externRevision: null,
    });
  });

  test("sparar flera längdrader för samma fysiska paket", async () => {
    const payload = createPayload({
      langder: [
        { antal: 440, langdDm: 48, m3: 2.64, actM3: 2.5344, lpm: 2112 },
        { antal: 120, langdDm: 54, m3: 0.81, actM3: 0.7776, lpm: 648 },
      ],
    });

    const result = await postPaket(payload);
    expect(result.status).toBe(201);
    expect(result.data.result).toBe("created");
    expect(result.data.rows).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(new Set(result.data.rows.map((row) => row.pktNr))).toEqual(
      new Set([payload.pktNr]),
    );
  });

  test.each(["Imp", "imp", "IMP", "Impregnerad", "impregnerad"])(
    "normaliserar behandlingen %s till Impregnerad utan att ändra träslag",
    async (incomingBehandling) => {
      const payload = createPayload({
        traslag: "Furu",
        behandling: incomingBehandling,
      });

      const result = await postPaket(payload);
      expect(result.status).toBe(201);
      expect(result.data.rows[0]).toMatchObject({
        behandling: "Impregnerad",
      });

      const rows = await db
        .select()
        .from(lagerPosterTable)
        .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, payload.sourceId as string));
      expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
      expect(rows[0]).toMatchObject({
        traslag: "Furu",
        behandling: "Impregnerad",
      });
    },
  );

  test("bevarar andra behandlingar oförändrade", async () => {
    const payload = createPayload({
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Grundmålad",
    });

    const result = await postPaket(payload);
    expect(result.status).toBe(201);
    expect(result.data.rows[0]?.behandling).toBe("Grundmålad");
  });

  test("visar behandling i detaljsvar och hittar paketet via behandling eller träslag", async () => {
    const payload = createPayload({
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Imp",
    });

    const created = await postPaket(payload);
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    const rowId = created.data.rows[0]?.id;
    expect(rowId).toBeTypeOf("number");

    const detailResponse = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/${rowId}`);
    expect(detailResponse.status).toBe(200);
    const detail = await detailResponse.json() as {
      id: number;
      traslag: string | null;
      behandling: string | null;
    };
    expect(detail).toMatchObject({
      id: rowId,
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
    });

    for (const search of ["imp", "impregnerad", "furu"]) {
      const searchResponse = await fetch(
        `${baseUrl}/?search=${encodeURIComponent(search)}&status=alla&limit=3000`,
      );
      expect(searchResponse.status).toBe(200);
      const result = await searchResponse.json() as {
        data: Array<{ id: number; traslag: string | null; behandling: string | null }>;
      };
      expect(result.data).toContainEqual(
        expect.objectContaining({
          id: rowId,
          traslag: "Furu",
          behandling: "Impregnerad",
        }),
      );
    }
  });

  test("behåller normaliserad behandling när en ny revision utelämnar fältet", async () => {
    const sourceId = randomUUID();
    sourceIds.push(sourceId);
    const initial = createPayload({
      sourceId,
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Imp",
    });
    expect((await postPaket(initial)).status).toBe(201);

    const { behandling: _utelamnadBehandling, ...revisionWithoutBehandling } = initial;
    const updated = await postPaket({
      ...revisionWithoutBehandling,
      clientRequestId: randomUUID(),
      revision: 2,
    });
    expect(updated.status).toBe(200);
    expect(updated.data.rows[0]).toMatchObject({
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
    });

    const activeRows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, sourceId));
    expect(activeRows.filter((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(activeRows.find((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toMatchObject({
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      externRevision: 2,
    });
  });

  test("hanterar utförande additivt och separat vid Paketlapp-upsert", async () => {
    const sourceId = randomUUID();
    sourceIds.push(sourceId);
    const initial = createPayload({
      sourceId,
      traslag: "Gran",
      behandling: "Imp",
      utforande: "buntat",
    });

    const created = await postPaket(initial);
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    expect(created.data.rows[0]).toMatchObject({
      traslag: "Gran",
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      utforande: "Buntat",
    });

    const { utforande: _utelamnatUtforande, ...withoutUtforande } = initial;
    const preserved = await postPaket({
      ...withoutUtforande,
      clientRequestId: randomUUID(),
      revision: 2,
    });
    expect(preserved.status).toBe(200);
    expect(preserved.data.rows[0]).toMatchObject({
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      utforande: "Buntat",
    });

    const normalized = await postPaket({
      ...withoutUtforande,
      clientRequestId: randomUUID(),
      revision: 3,
      utforande: "4 RH",
    });
    expect(normalized.status).toBe(200);
    expect(normalized.data.rows[0]).toMatchObject({
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      utforande: "4RH",
    });

    const cleared = await postPaket({
      ...withoutUtforande,
      clientRequestId: randomUUID(),
      revision: 4,
      utforande: null,
    });
    expect(cleared.status).toBe(200);
    expect(cleared.data.rows[0]).toMatchObject({
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      utforande: null,
    });

    const activeRows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, sourceId));
    expect(activeRows.find((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toMatchObject({
      traslag: "Gran",
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      utforande: null,
      externRevision: 4,
    });
  });

  test("sparar ingenting när en längdrad är ogiltig", async () => {
    const payload = createPayload({
      langder: [
        { antal: 100, langdDm: 48, m3: 1, actM3: 0.96, lpm: 480 },
        { antal: 0, langdDm: 54, m3: 1, actM3: 0.96, lpm: 540 },
      ],
    });

    const result = await postPaket<{ error: string }>(payload);
    expect(result.status).toBe(400);

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, payload.sourceId as string));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  test("rullar tillbaka första längdraden om en senare databasinsert misslyckas", async () => {
    const sourceId = randomUUID();
    sourceIds.push(sourceId);
    const functionName = `paketlapp_fail_${Date.now()}_${process.pid}`;
    const triggerName = `${functionName}_trigger`;
    const safeSourceId = sourceId.replaceAll("'", "''");

    await db.execute(sql.raw(`
      CREATE FUNCTION ${functionName}() RETURNS trigger AS $$
      BEGIN
        IF NEW.extern_source_id = '${safeSourceId}' AND NEW.langd_dm::numeric = 54 THEN
          RAISE EXCEPTION 'avsiktligt Paketlapp-testfel';
        END IF;
        RETURN NEW;
      END;
      $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql
    `));
    await db.execute(sql.raw(`
      CREATE TRIGGER ${triggerName}
      BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON lager_poster
      FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION ${functionName}()
    `));

    try {
      const payload = createPayload({
        sourceId,
        langder: [
          { antal: 100, langdDm: 48, m3: 1, actM3: 0.96, lpm: 480 },
          { antal: 100, langdDm: 54, m3: 1, actM3: 0.96, lpm: 540 },
        ],
      });
      const result = await postPaket<{ error: string }>(payload);
      expect(result.status).toBe(500);

      const rows = await db
        .select()
        .from(lagerPosterTable)
        .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, sourceId));
      expect(rows).toHaveLength(0);
    } finally {
      await db.execute(sql.raw(`DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS ${triggerName} ON lager_poster`));
      await db.execute(sql.raw(`DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS ${functionName}()`));
    }
  });

  test("returnerar tidigare resultat vid idempotent retry", async () => {
    const payload = createPayload({
      langder: [
        { antal: 30, langdDm: 42, m3: 0.4, actM3: 0.38, lpm: 126 },
        { antal: 20, langdDm: 48, m3: 0.3, actM3: 0.28, lpm: 96 },
      ],
    });
    const idempotencyKey = randomUUID();

    const first = await postPaket(payload, idempotencyKey);
    const retry = await postPaket(payload, idempotencyKey);
    expect(first.status).toBe(201);
    expect(retry.status).toBe(200);
    expect(retry.data).toEqual(first.data);

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, payload.sourceId as string));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
  });

  test("serialiserar samtidiga retries med samma idempotensnyckel", async () => {
    const payload = createPayload();
    const idempotencyKey = randomUUID();
    const [first, second] = await Promise.all([
      postPaket(payload, idempotencyKey),
      postPaket(payload, idempotencyKey),
    ]);

    expect([first.status, second.status].sort()).toEqual([200, 201]);
    expect(first.data).toEqual(second.data);

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, payload.sourceId as string));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  test("returnerar 409 och sparar inga rader vid pktNr-konflikt", async () => {
    const firstPayload = createPayload();
    const first = await postPaket(firstPayload);
    expect(first.status).toBe(201);

    const conflictingPayload = createPayload({
      pktNr: firstPayload.pktNr,
      langder: [
        { antal: 10, langdDm: 42, m3: 0.2, actM3: 0.19, lpm: 42 },
        { antal: 10, langdDm: 48, m3: 0.2, actM3: 0.19, lpm: 48 },
      ],
    });
    const conflict = await postPaket<{ error: string }>(conflictingPayload);
    expect(conflict.status).toBe(409);

    const conflictingRows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(
        eq(
          lagerPosterTable.externSourceId,
          conflictingPayload.sourceId as string,
        ),
      );
    expect(conflictingRows).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  test("uppdaterar samma sourceId atomiskt med en ny revision", async () => {
    const sourceId = randomUUID();
    sourceIds.push(sourceId);
    const pktNr = `TEST-UPDATE-${suffix}-${randomUUID()}`;
    const initial = createPayload({
      sourceId,
      pktNr,
      langder: [
        { antal: 100, langdDm: 42, m3: 1, actM3: 0.96, lpm: 420 },
        { antal: 100, langdDm: 48, m3: 1.1, actM3: 1.05, lpm: 480 },
      ],
    });
    const created = await postPaket(initial);
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);

    const updatedPayload = {
      ...initial,
      clientRequestId: randomUUID(),
      revision: 2,
      behandling: "",
      langder: [
        { antal: 75, langdDm: 54, m3: 0.9, actM3: 0.86, lpm: 405 },
      ],
    };
    const updated = await postPaket(updatedPayload);
    expect(updated.status).toBe(200);
    expect(updated.data).toMatchObject({
      sourceId,
      revision: 2,
      result: "updated",
      pktNr,
    });
    expect(updated.data.rows).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(updated.data.rows[0]).toMatchObject({
      behandling: null,
      langdDm: 54,
      antal: 75,
    });

    const activeRows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(
        eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, sourceId),
      );
    expect(activeRows.filter((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(activeRows.filter((row) => row.raderadAt !== null)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(
      activeRows.filter((row) => row.raderadAt === null)[0]?.externRevision,
    ).toBe(2);

    expect(
      activeRows.filter((row) => row.raderadAt === null)[0]?.behandling,
    ).toBeNull();
  });

  test("avregistrerar alla längdrader atomiskt med sourceId", async () => {
    const initial = createPayload({
      langder: [
        { antal: 30, langdDm: 42, m3: 0.4, actM3: 0.38, lpm: 126 },
        { antal: 20, langdDm: 48, m3: 0.3, actM3: 0.28, lpm: 96 },
      ],
    });
    const created = await postPaket(initial);
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);

    const deleted = await postPaket(
      createDeletePayload(
        initial.sourceId as string,
        initial.pktNr as string,
      ),
    );
    expect(deleted.status).toBe(200);
    expect(deleted.data).toMatchObject({
      sourceId: initial.sourceId,
      revision: 2,
      operation: "delete",
      result: "deleted",
      pktNr: initial.pktNr,
    });
    expect(deleted.data.rows).toHaveLength(2);

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, initial.sourceId as string));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(rows.every((row) => row.raderadAt !== null)).toBe(true);
  });

  test("rullar tillbaka alla avregistreringar om en längdrad misslyckas", async () => {
    const initial = createPayload({
      langder: [
        { antal: 30, langdDm: 42, m3: 0.4, actM3: 0.38, lpm: 126 },
        { antal: 20, langdDm: 48, m3: 0.3, actM3: 0.28, lpm: 96 },
      ],
    });
    expect((await postPaket(initial)).status).toBe(201);

    const before = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, initial.sourceId as string));
    expect(before).toHaveLength(2);

    const functionName = `paketlapp_delete_fail_${Date.now()}_${process.pid}`;
    const triggerName = `${functionName}_trigger`;
    const failingRowId = before[1]!.id;
    await db.execute(sql.raw(`
      CREATE FUNCTION ${functionName}() RETURNS trigger AS $$
      BEGIN
        IF OLD.id = ${failingRowId} AND NEW.raderad_at IS NOT NULL THEN
          RAISE EXCEPTION 'avsiktligt Paketlapp-delete-testfel';
        END IF;
        RETURN NEW;
      END;
      $$ LANGUAGE plpgsql
    `));
    await db.execute(sql.raw(`
      CREATE TRIGGER ${triggerName}
      BEFORE UPDATE ON lager_poster
      FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION ${functionName}()
    `));

    try {
      const result = await postPaket<{ error: string }>(
        createDeletePayload(
          initial.sourceId as string,
          initial.pktNr as string,
        ),
      );
      expect(result.status).toBe(500);

      const after = await db
        .select()
        .from(lagerPosterTable)
        .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, initial.sourceId as string));
      expect(after).toHaveLength(2);
      expect(after.every((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toBe(true);
    } finally {
      await db.execute(sql.raw(`DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS ${triggerName} ON lager_poster`));
      await db.execute(sql.raw(`DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS ${functionName}()`));
    }
  });

  test("returnerar samma deleted-svar vid säker idempotent retry", async () => {
    const initial = createPayload();
    expect((await postPaket(initial)).status).toBe(201);

    const payload = createDeletePayload(
      initial.sourceId as string,
      initial.pktNr as string,
    );
    const idempotencyKey = randomUUID();
    const first = await postPaket(payload, idempotencyKey);
    const retry = await postPaket(payload, idempotencyKey);

    expect(first.status).toBe(200);
    expect(first.data.result).toBe("deleted");
    expect(retry.status).toBe(200);
    expect(retry.data).toEqual(first.data);
  });

  test("returnerar 404 för okänt sourceId även om pktNr finns", async () => {
    const initial = createPayload();
    expect((await postPaket(initial)).status).toBe(201);

    const unknownSourceId = randomUUID();
    sourceIds.push(unknownSourceId);
    const result = await postPaket<{ error: string }>(
      createDeletePayload(
        unknownSourceId,
        initial.pktNr as string,
      ),
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(404);

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, initial.sourceId as string));
    expect(rows.every((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  test("returnerar 409 vid gammal delete-revision utan att ändra paketet", async () => {
    const initial = createPayload();
    expect((await postPaket(initial)).status).toBe(201);

    const result = await postPaket<{ error: string }>(
      createDeletePayload(
        initial.sourceId as string,
        initial.pktNr as string,
        { revision: 1 },
      ),
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(409);

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, initial.sourceId as string));
    expect(rows.every((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  test("returnerar 409 vid sourceId/pktNr-konflikt utan att ändra paketet", async () => {
    const initial = createPayload();
    expect((await postPaket(initial)).status).toBe(201);

    const result = await postPaket<{ error: string }>(
      createDeletePayload(
        initial.sourceId as string,
        `FEL-${initial.pktNr as string}`,
      ),
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(409);

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, initial.sourceId as string));
    expect(rows.every((row) => row.raderadAt === null)).toBe(true);
  });

  test("avregistrerar aldrig paket från annan produktion", async () => {
    const sourceId = randomUUID();
    sourceIds.push(sourceId);
    const pktNr = `ANNAN-PRODUKTION-${suffix}-${randomUUID()}`;
    await db.insert(lagerPosterTable).values({
      pktNr,
      produktion: "ANNAN_LEVERANTOR",
      externSourceId: sourceId,
      externRevision: 1,
      antal: 10,
      langdDm: "42",
    });

    const result = await postPaket<{ error: string }>(
      createDeletePayload(sourceId, pktNr),
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(409);

    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.externSourceId, sourceId));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(rows[0]?.raderadAt).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe("Manuell behandling på lagerpost", () => {
  test("normaliserar Imp och Grundmålat utan att ändra träslag, och tillåter tom behandling", async () => {
    const cases = [
      { suffix: "imp", traslag: "Furu", behandling: "Imp", expected: "Impregnerad" },
      { suffix: "grund", traslag: "Gran", behandling: "Grundmålat", expected: "Grundmålad" },
      { suffix: "tom", traslag: "Furu", behandling: "   ", expected: null },
    ] as const;

    for (const testCase of cases) {
      const created = await requestLager<{
        id: number;
        traslag: string | null;
        behandling: string | null;
      }>("POST", "/", {
        pktNr: `TEST-MAN-${suffix}-${testCase.suffix}`,
        profil: "Regel",
        traslag: testCase.traslag,
        behandling: testCase.behandling,
      });

      expect(created.status).toBe(201);
      directPostIds.push(created.data.id);
      expect(created.data).toMatchObject({
        traslag: testCase.traslag,
        behandling: testCase.expected,
      });
    }
  });

  test("redigering bevarar utelämnad behandling och normaliserar ett nytt värde", async () => {
    const created = await requestLager<{
      id: number;
      traslag: string | null;
      behandling: string | null;
    }>("POST", "/", {
      pktNr: `TEST-MAN-${suffix}-edit`,
      profil: "Regel",
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Imp",
    });
    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    directPostIds.push(created.data.id);

    const preserved = await requestLager<{
      id: number;
      traslag: string | null;
      behandling: string | null;
    }>("PUT", `/${created.data.id}`, { kvalitet: "C24" });
    expect(preserved.status).toBe(200);
    expect(preserved.data).toMatchObject({
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
    });

    const updated = await requestLager<{
      id: number;
      traslag: string | null;
      behandling: string | null;
    }>("PUT", `/${created.data.id}`, { behandling: "  Egen behandling  " });
    expect(updated.status).toBe(200);
    expect(updated.data).toMatchObject({
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Egen behandling",
    });
  });

  test("duplicering behåller behandling och behandlingsalias fungerar i sökningen", async () => {
    const source = await requestLager<{
      id: number;
      pktNr: string | null;
      traslag: string | null;
      behandling: string | null;
      skapadAt: string;
      uppdateradAt: string;
    }>("POST", "/", {
      pktNr: `TEST-MAN-${suffix}-source`,
      profil: "Regel",
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Imp",
    });
    expect(source.status).toBe(201);
    directPostIds.push(source.data.id);

    const {
      id: _id,
      skapadAt: _skapadAt,
      uppdateradAt: _uppdateradAt,
      ...duplicateBody
    } = source.data;
    const duplicate = await requestLager<{
      id: number;
      traslag: string | null;
      behandling: string | null;
    }>("POST", "/", {
      ...duplicateBody,
      pktNr: `TEST-MAN-${suffix}-copy`,
    });
    expect(duplicate.status).toBe(201);
    directPostIds.push(duplicate.data.id);
    expect(duplicate.data).toMatchObject({
      traslag: "Furu",
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
    });

    const ground = await requestLager<{ id: number }>("POST", "/", {
      pktNr: `TEST-MAN-${suffix}-search-ground`,
      profil: "Panel",
      traslag: "Gran",
      behandling: "grundmålat",
    });
    expect(ground.status).toBe(201);
    directPostIds.push(ground.data.id);

    for (const [term, expectedId] of [
      ["imp", source.data.id],
      ["impregnerad", source.data.id],
      ["grund", ground.data.id],
      ["grundmålat", ground.data.id],
    ] as const) {
      const result = await requestLager<{
        data: Array<{ id: number }>;
      }>("GET", `/?search=${encodeURIComponent(term)}&status=alla&limit=3000`);
      expect(result.status).toBe(200);
      expect(result.data.data.map((row) => row.id)).toContain(expectedId);
    }
  });
});

describe("Externt lager-API", () => {
  test("normaliserar, returnerar och håller utförande separat från övriga produktegenskaper", async () => {
    const pktNr = `TEST-EXT-UTF-${suffix}`;
    const created = await requestExternLager<{
      id: number;
      pktNr: string | null;
      profil: string | null;
      traslag: string | null;
      kvalitet: string | null;
      behandling: string | null;
      utforande: string | null;
    }>("POST", "", {
      pktNr,
      profil: "Regel",
      traslag: "Furu",
      kvalitet: "C24",
      behandling: "Grundmålat",
      utforande: "planhyvlat",
    });

    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    directPostIds.push(created.data.id);
    expect(created.data).toMatchObject({
      profil: "Regel",
      traslag: "Furu",
      kvalitet: "C24",
      behandling: "Grundmålad",
      utforande: "Planhyvlat",
    });

    const fetched = await requestExternLager<typeof created.data>(
      "GET",
      `/${encodeURIComponent(pktNr)}`,
    );
    expect(fetched.status).toBe(200);
    expect(fetched.data).toMatchObject({
      behandling: "Grundmålad",
      utforande: "Planhyvlat",
    });
  });
});

describe("Separat utförande på lagerpost", () => {
  test("normaliserar och sparar utförande utan att påverka behandling eller träslag", async () => {
    const created = await requestLager<{
      id: number;
      traslag: string | null;
      behandling: string | null;
      utforande: string | null;
    }>("POST", "/", {
      pktNr: `TEST-UTF-${suffix}-create`,
      profil: "Tegelläkt",
      traslag: "Gran",
      behandling: "Imp",
      utforande: "buntat",
    });

    expect(created.status).toBe(201);
    directPostIds.push(created.data.id);
    expect(created.data).toMatchObject({
      traslag: "Gran",
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      utforande: "Buntat",
    });

    const updated = await requestLager<{
      id: number;
      behandling: string | null;
      utforande: string | null;
    }>("PUT", `/${created.data.id}`, { utforande: "4 rh" });
    expect(updated.status).toBe(200);
    expect(updated.data).toMatchObject({
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      utforande: "4RH",
    });

    const cleared = await requestLager<{
      id: number;
      behandling: string | null;
      utforande: string | null;
    }>("PUT", `/${created.data.id}`, { utforande: "   " });
    expect(cleared.status).toBe(200);
    expect(cleared.data).toMatchObject({
      behandling: "Impregnerad",
      utforande: null,
    });
  });

  test("duplicering behåller utförande och sökning hittar normaliserade värden", async () => {
    const cases = [
      { suffix: "bunt", value: "Buntat", terms: ["bunt"] },
      { suffix: "plan", value: "planhyvlat", terms: ["planhyv"] },
      { suffix: "rh", value: "4 RH", terms: ["4rh"] },
    ] as const;

    for (const testCase of cases) {
      const source = await requestLager<{
        id: number;
        pktNr: string | null;
        traslag: string | null;
        behandling: string | null;
        utforande: string | null;
        skapadAt: string;
        uppdateradAt: string;
      }>("POST", "/", {
        pktNr: `TEST-UTF-${suffix}-${testCase.suffix}`,
        profil: "Regel",
        traslag: "Gran",
        utforande: testCase.value,
      });
      expect(source.status).toBe(201);
      directPostIds.push(source.data.id);

      for (const term of testCase.terms) {
        const result = await requestLager<{ data: Array<{ id: number }> }>(
          "GET",
          `/?search=${encodeURIComponent(term)}&status=alla&limit=3000`,
        );
        expect(result.status).toBe(200);
        expect(result.data.data.map((row) => row.id)).toContain(source.data.id);
      }

      if (testCase.suffix === "bunt") {
        const {
          id: _id,
          skapadAt: _skapadAt,
          uppdateradAt: _uppdateradAt,
          ...duplicateBody
        } = source.data;
        const duplicate = await requestLager<{ id: number; utforande: string | null }>(
          "POST",
          "/",
          { ...duplicateBody, pktNr: `TEST-UTF-${suffix}-copy` },
        );
        expect(duplicate.status).toBe(201);
        directPostIds.push(duplicate.data.id);
        expect(duplicate.data.utforande).toBe("Buntat");
      }
    }
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/__tests__/specDocumentFields.test.ts ================

import "../../app";

import express from "express";
import type { Server } from "node:http";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db, pool, specifikationerTable } from "@workspace/db";
import specRouter from "../spec";

let server: Server;
let baseUrl: string;
let specId: number | null = null;

async function request<T>(path: string, method = "GET", body?: unknown) {
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
    method,
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  return { status: response.status, data: await response.json() as T };
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json());
  app.use("/spec", specRouter);
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server = app.listen(0, (error?: Error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve());
  });
  const address = server.address();
  if (!address || typeof address === "string") throw new Error("Testserver kunde inte startas");
  baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`;
});

afterAll(async () => {
  if (specId !== null) {
    await db.delete(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, specId));
  }
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.close(() => resolve()));
  await pool.end();
});

describe("separata dokumentfält", () => {
  test("ordernummer, Er referens och anteckning sparas och läses separat", async () => {
    const created = await request<{ id: number }>("/spec", "POST", {
      typ: "leverans",
      kund: "TEST DOKUMENTFÄLT",
      orderNr: "456789",
      erReferens: "Mikael Andersson, 0730-611166",
      anteckning: "Ring före leverans",
      paketIds: [],
    });
    expect(created.status).toBe(200);
    specId = created.data.id;

    const loaded = await request<{
      orderNr: string | null;
      erReferens: string | null;
      anteckning: string | null;
    }>(`/spec/${specId}`);
    expect(loaded.status).toBe(200);
    expect(loaded.data).toMatchObject({
      orderNr: "456789",
      erReferens: "Mikael Andersson, 0730-611166",
      anteckning: "Ring före leverans",
    });
  });

  test("äldre PUT-klienter bevarar de nya additiva fälten", async () => {
    if (specId === null) throw new Error("Testspec saknas");
    const updated = await request<{ id: number }>(`/spec/${specId}`, "PUT", {
      typ: "leverans",
      kund: "TEST DOKUMENTFÄLT UPPDATERAD",
      orderNr: "456789",
      paketIds: [],
      certifikat: [],
    });
    expect(updated.status).toBe(200);

    const [stored] = await db
      .select({
        erReferens: specifikationerTable.erReferens,
        anteckning: specifikationerTable.anteckning,
      })
      .from(specifikationerTable)
      .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, specId));
    expect(stored).toEqual({
      erReferens: "Mikael Andersson, 0730-611166",
      anteckning: "Ring före leverans",
    });
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/__tests__/specScanImport.test.ts ================

import "../../app";

import express from "express";
import type { Server } from "node:http";
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, test } from "vitest";
import { eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import {
  db,
  lagerFackTable,
  lagerHistorikTable,
  lagerOmradenTable,
  lagerPosterTable,
  pool,
  specPaketTable,
  specifikationerTable,
} from "@workspace/db";
import specRouter from "../spec";
import omradenRouter from "../omraden";

const suffix = `${Date.now()}-${process.pid}`;
const postIds: number[] = [];
const specIds: number[] = [];
const omradeIds: number[] = [];
const fackIds: number[] = [];
let server: Server;
let baseUrl: string;
let omradeA: typeof lagerOmradenTable.$inferSelect;
let omradeB: typeof lagerOmradenTable.$inferSelect;
let omradeUtanFack: typeof lagerOmradenTable.$inferSelect;
let fackA: typeof lagerFackTable.$inferSelect;
let fackB: typeof lagerFackTable.$inferSelect;

async function api<T>(path: string, body?: unknown): Promise<{ status: number; data: T }> {
  const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}${path}`, {
    method: body === undefined ? "GET" : "POST",
    headers: { "content-type": "application/json", "x-test-user": "spec-import-test" },
    body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body),
  });
  const text = await response.text();
  return { status: response.status, data: (text ? JSON.parse(text) : null) as T };
}

function paket(
  pktNr: string,
  placement: { omradeId?: number | null; fackId?: number | null } = {},
) {
  return {
    pktNr,
    traslag: "Gran",
    profil: "Klädsel",
    kvalitet: "G4-2",
    behandling: "Grundmålat",
    utforande: "4 rh",
    nomTjocklek: 22,
    nomBredd: 145,
    langdDm: 42,
    antal: 10,
    m3Pris: 1200,
    m3Sum: 160.78,
    ...placement,
  };
}

async function importera(
  name: string,
  packages: ReturnType<typeof paket>[],
) {
  const result = await api<{ specId: number; antalPaket: number; error?: string }>(
    "/spec/scan-import",
    { avsandare: name, datum: "2026-09-04", paket: packages },
  );
  if (result.status === 200) {
    specIds.push(result.data.specId);
    const rows = await db
      .select({ id: lagerPosterTable.id })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, packages.map((item) => item.pktNr)));
    postIds.push(...rows.map((row) => row.id));
  }
  return result;
}

beforeAll(async () => {
  const app = express();
  app.use(express.json());
  app.use((req, _res, next) => {
    (req as express.Request & { userId?: string }).userId = String(req.header("x-test-user") ?? "test");
    next();
  });
  app.use("/spec", specRouter);
  app.use("/lager/omraden", omradenRouter);
  await new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
    server = app.listen(0, (error?: Error) => error ? reject(error) : resolve());
  });
  const address = server.address();
  if (!address || typeof address === "string") throw new Error("Testserver kunde inte startas");
  baseUrl = `http://127.0.0.1:${address.port}`;

  [omradeA] = await db.insert(lagerOmradenTable).values({
    namn: `TEST_SPEC_IMPORT_A_${suffix}`,
    slug: `test-spec-import-a-${suffix}`,
  }).returning();
  [omradeB] = await db.insert(lagerOmradenTable).values({
    namn: `TEST_SPEC_IMPORT_B_${suffix}`,
    slug: `test-spec-import-b-${suffix}`,
  }).returning();
  [omradeUtanFack] = await db.insert(lagerOmradenTable).values({
    namn: `TEST_SPEC_IMPORT_TOM_${suffix}`,
    slug: `test-spec-import-tom-${suffix}`,
  }).returning();
  omradeIds.push(omradeA.id, omradeB.id, omradeUtanFack.id);

  [fackA] = await db.insert(lagerFackTable).values({ omradeId: omradeA.id, kod: "V01" }).returning();
  [fackB] = await db.insert(lagerFackTable).values({ omradeId: omradeB.id, kod: "H01" }).returning();
  fackIds.push(fackA.id, fackB.id);
});

afterAll(async () => {
  if (postIds.length > 0) {
    await db.delete(lagerHistorikTable).where(inArray(lagerHistorikTable.lagerId, postIds));
  }
  if (specIds.length > 0) {
    await db.delete(specPaketTable).where(inArray(specPaketTable.specId, specIds));
    await db.delete(specifikationerTable).where(inArray(specifikationerTable.id, specIds));
  }
  if (postIds.length > 0) {
    await db.delete(lagerPosterTable).where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.id, postIds));
  }
  if (fackIds.length > 0) await db.delete(lagerFackTable).where(inArray(lagerFackTable.id, fackIds));
  if (omradeIds.length > 0) await db.delete(lagerOmradenTable).where(inArray(lagerOmradenTable.id, omradeIds));
  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => server.close(() => resolve()));
  await pool.end();
});

describe("dokumentimport med strukturerad lagerplats", () => {
  test("alla paket får standardområde och standardfack samt blir aktuellt lager", async () => {
    const pktNrs = [`TEST-DEFAULT-1-${suffix}`, `TEST-DEFAULT-2-${suffix}`];
    const result = await importera(
      `TEST_DEFAULT_${suffix}`,
      pktNrs.map((pktNr) => paket(pktNr, { omradeId: omradeA.id, fackId: fackA.id })),
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(200);
    expect(result.data.antalPaket).toBe(2);

    const rows = await db.select().from(lagerPosterTable).where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNrs));
    expect(rows).toHaveLength(2);
    expect(rows.every((row) =>
      row.omradeId === omradeA.id &&
      row.fackId === fackA.id &&
      row.plats === omradeA.namn &&
      row.avrappTyp === null
    )).toBe(true);
    expect(rows[0].behandling).toBe("Grundmålad");
    expect(rows[0].utforande).toBe("4RH");
    expect(rows[0].m3Pris).toBe("1200");

    const detail = await api<{ fack: Array<{ id: number; posterAntal: number }> }>(
      `/lager/omraden/${omradeA.slug}`,
    );
    expect(detail.status).toBe(200);
    expect(detail.data.fack.find((item) => item.id === fackA.id)?.posterAntal).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(2);
  });

  test("individuella paket kan placeras i olika område och fack", async () => {
    const pktA = `TEST-IND-A-${suffix}`;
    const pktB = `TEST-IND-B-${suffix}`;
    const result = await importera(`TEST_IND_${suffix}`, [
      paket(pktA, { omradeId: omradeA.id, fackId: fackA.id }),
      paket(pktB, { omradeId: omradeB.id, fackId: fackB.id }),
    ]);
    expect(result.status).toBe(200);
    const rows = await db.select().from(lagerPosterTable).where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, [pktA, pktB]));
    expect(rows.find((row) => row.pktNr === pktA)).toMatchObject({ omradeId: omradeA.id, fackId: fackA.id });
    expect(rows.find((row) => row.pktNr === pktB)).toMatchObject({ omradeId: omradeB.id, fackId: fackB.id });
  });

  test("område utan fack och ingen lagerplats fungerar", async () => {
    const pktOmrade = `TEST-NOFACK-${suffix}`;
    const pktIngen = `TEST-NONE-${suffix}`;
    expect((await importera(`TEST_NOFACK_${suffix}`, [
      paket(pktOmrade, { omradeId: omradeUtanFack.id, fackId: null }),
    ])).status).toBe(200);
    expect((await importera(`TEST_NONE_${suffix}`, [paket(pktIngen)])).status).toBe(200);

    const rows = await db.select().from(lagerPosterTable).where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, [pktOmrade, pktIngen]));
    expect(rows.find((row) => row.pktNr === pktOmrade)).toMatchObject({
      omradeId: omradeUtanFack.id,
      fackId: null,
      plats: omradeUtanFack.namn,
    });
    expect(rows.find((row) => row.pktNr === pktIngen)).toMatchObject({
      omradeId: null,
      fackId: null,
      plats: null,
    });
  });

  test("ogiltigt område/fack-par stoppar hela importen före alla skrivningar", async () => {
    const pktNrs = [`TEST-ATOMIC-1-${suffix}`, `TEST-ATOMIC-2-${suffix}`];
    const sender = `TEST_ATOMIC_${suffix}`;
    const result = await importera(sender, [
      paket(pktNrs[0], { omradeId: omradeA.id, fackId: fackA.id }),
      paket(pktNrs[1], { omradeId: omradeA.id, fackId: fackB.id }),
    ]);
    expect(result.status).toBe(400);
    expect(result.data.error).toContain("tillhör inte valt område");
    expect(await db.select().from(lagerPosterTable).where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNrs))).toHaveLength(0);
    expect(await db.select().from(specifikationerTable).where(eq(specifikationerTable.kund, sender))).toHaveLength(0);
  });

  test("databasen nekar ett fack under fel område även utan API-valideringen", async () => {
    await expect(db.insert(lagerPosterTable).values({
      pktNr: `TEST-DB-CONSTRAINT-${suffix}`,
      omradeId: omradeA.id,
      fackId: fackB.id,
    })).rejects.toThrow();
  });

  test("befintlig importpayload utan plats fortsätter fungera", async () => {
    const result = await importera(
      `TEST_LEGACY_${suffix}`,
      [paket(`TEST-LEGACY-${suffix}`)],
    );
    expect(result.status).toBe(200);
    expect(result.data.antalPaket).toBe(1);
  });
});

================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/admin.ts ================

import { Router } from "express";
import { clerkClient } from "@clerk/express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { tillatnaEmailsTable } from "@workspace/db/schema";
import { ExecuteImpMigrationBody } from "@workspace/api-zod";
import {
  dryRunImpTillFuru,
  executeImpTillFuru,
} from "../lib/impTillFuruMigration";

const router = Router();

router.get("/data-migrations/imp-to-furu", async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    return res.json(await dryRunImpTillFuru());
  } catch {
    return res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte genomföra dry-run" });
  }
});

router.post("/data-migrations/imp-to-furu", async (req: any, res) => {
  const parsed = ExecuteImpMigrationBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bekräftelsen är ogiltig" });
  }
  try {
    return res.json(await executeImpTillFuru(req.userId, parsed.data.confirmation));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof Error && error.message === "confirmation_mismatch") {
      return res.status(400).json({ error: "Bekräftelsetexten stämmer inte" });
    }
    return res.status(500).json({ error: "Migreringen misslyckades och återställdes" });
  }
});

router.get("/me", async (req: any, res) => {
  try {
    const userId = req.userId;
    if (!userId) return res.status(401).json({ error: "Ej inloggad" });
    const user = await clerkClient.users.getUser(userId);
    res.json({
      id: user.id,
      email: user.emailAddresses[0]?.emailAddress ?? null,
      firstName: user.firstName,
      lastName: user.lastName,
      role: (user.publicMetadata?.role as string) ?? null,
    });
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte hämta användare" });
  }
});

router.get("/users", async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    const response = await clerkClient.users.getUserList({ limit: 100, orderBy: "-created_at" });
    const users = response.data.map((u) => ({
      id: u.id,
      email: u.emailAddresses[0]?.emailAddress ?? null,
      firstName: u.firstName,
      lastName: u.lastName,
      lastSignInAt: u.lastSignInAt,
      lastActiveAt: u.lastActiveAt ?? null,
      createdAt: u.createdAt,
      banned: u.banned,
      role: (u.publicMetadata?.role as string) ?? null,
    }));
    res.json(users);
  } catch (err) {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte hämta användare" });
  }
});

router.post("/invite", async (req: any, res) => {
  const { email, rol } = req.body as { email?: string; rol?: string };
  if (!email || !email.includes("@")) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltig e-postadress" });
  }
  const normalized = email.toLowerCase().trim();
  const grantRole = (rol === "admin" || rol === "anvandare") ? rol : "anvandare";
  try {
    const adminUser = req.userId ? await clerkClient.users.getUser(req.userId) : null;
    const addedBy = adminUser?.emailAddresses[0]?.emailAddress ?? null;
    await db
      .insert(tillatnaEmailsTable)
      .values({ email: normalized, rol: grantRole, lagtTillAv: addedBy })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: tillatnaEmailsTable.email, set: { rol: grantRole } });
  } catch {
    // allowlist insert failure is non-fatal
  }
  // Sync to Clerk's own allowlist so sign-up is blocked for non-approved emails
  // (requires "Restrictions → Allowlist" to be enabled in Clerk dashboard)
  try {
    await clerkClient.allowlistIdentifiers.createAllowlistIdentifier({
      identifier: normalized,
      notify: false,
    });
  } catch {
    // Silently ignore — feature may not be enabled in Clerk dashboard yet
  }
  res.json({ ok: true });
});

router.put("/users/:userId/role", async (req, res) => {
  const { userId } = req.params;
  const { role } = req.body as { role?: string };
  const validRoles = ["admin", "anvandare"];
  if (role && !validRoles.includes(role)) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltig roll" });
  }
  try {
    await clerkClient.users.updateUser(userId, {
      publicMetadata: { role: role || null },
    });
    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte uppdatera roll" });
  }
});

router.delete("/users/:userId", async (req, res) => {
  const { userId } = req.params;
  try {
    await clerkClient.users.deleteUser(userId);
    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte ta bort användare" });
  }
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/allowlist.ts ================

import { Router } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { tillatnaEmailsTable } from "@workspace/db/schema";
import { eq, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { requireAdmin } from "../app";

const router = Router();

router.get("/", requireAdmin, async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    const rows = await db
      .select()
      .from(tillatnaEmailsTable)
      .orderBy(tillatnaEmailsTable.lagtTillAt);
    res.json(rows);
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte hämta listan" });
  }
});

router.post("/", requireAdmin, async (req, res) => {
  const { email, lagtTillAv, rol } = req.body as { email?: string; lagtTillAv?: string; rol?: string };
  if (!email || !email.includes("@")) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltig e-postadress" });
  }
  const normalized = email.toLowerCase().trim();
  const grantRole = (rol === "admin" || rol === "anvandare") ? rol : "anvandare";
  try {
    await db
      .insert(tillatnaEmailsTable)
      .values({ email: normalized, rol: grantRole, lagtTillAv: lagtTillAv ?? null })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({ target: tillatnaEmailsTable.email, set: { rol: grantRole } });
    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte lägga till e-post" });
  }
});

router.delete("/:email", requireAdmin, async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const email = decodeURIComponent(req.params.email).toLowerCase().trim();
  try {
    const result = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
      await tx.execute(sql`SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('tillatna_emails_delete'))`);
      const [{ count }] = await tx
        .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` })
        .from(tillatnaEmailsTable);
      const [existing] = await tx
        .select({ id: tillatnaEmailsTable.id })
        .from(tillatnaEmailsTable)
        .where(eq(tillatnaEmailsTable.email, email))
        .limit(1);

      if (!existing) return "missing" as const;
      if (Number(count) <= 1) return "last" as const;

      await tx.delete(tillatnaEmailsTable).where(eq(tillatnaEmailsTable.email, email));
      return "deleted" as const;
    });
    if (result === "last") {
      res.status(409).json({ error: "Den sista tillåtna e-postadressen kan inte tas bort" });
      return;
    }
    if (result === "missing") {
      res.status(404).json({ error: "E-postadressen hittades inte" });
      return;
    }
    res.json({ ok: true });
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte ta bort e-post" });
  }
});

router.get("/count", async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    const result = await db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` }).from(tillatnaEmailsTable);
    res.json({ count: result[0].count });
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ count: 0 });
  }
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/branding.ts ================

import { Router } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { brandingSettingsTable } from "@workspace/db/schema";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import sharp from "sharp";
import { requireAdmin } from "../app";
import { ObjectNotFoundError, ObjectStorageService } from "../lib/objectStorage";
import { brandingToDocumentProfile, profileResponse } from "../lib/documentProfile";

const router = Router();
const objectStorageService = new ObjectStorageService();

async function getSettings() {
  const [row] = await db.select().from(brandingSettingsTable).where(eq(brandingSettingsTable.id, 1)).limit(1);
  if (row) return row;
  const [created] = await db.insert(brandingSettingsTable).values({ id: 1 }).returning();
  return created;
}

function toResponse(row: typeof brandingSettingsTable.$inferSelect) {
  return {
    ...profileResponse(brandingToDocumentProfile(row)),
    updatedAt: row.updatedAt.toISOString(),
  };
}

function iconSize(value: unknown): number {
  const requested = Number(value);
  return [180, 192, 512].includes(requested) ? requested : 512;
}

router.get("/icon", async (req, res) => {
  const size = iconSize(req.query.size);

  try {
    const settings = await getSettings();
    if (!settings.logoPath) {
      const fallbackSize = size === 512 ? 512 : 192;
      res.redirect(`/pwa-${fallbackSize}.png?branding-icon=v2`);
      return;
    }
    if (!settings.logoPath.startsWith("/objects/")) {
      res.redirect(settings.logoPath);
      return;
    }

    const sourceResponse = await objectStorageService.downloadObject(
      await objectStorageService.getObjectEntityFile(settings.logoPath),
    );
    const source = Buffer.from(await sourceResponse.arrayBuffer());
    const margin = Math.round(size * 0.16);
    const innerSize = size - margin * 2;
    const icon = await sharp(source)
      .rotate()
      .resize({
        width: innerSize,
        height: innerSize,
        fit: "contain",
        background: { r: 255, g: 255, b: 255, alpha: 0 },
      })
      .extend({
        top: margin,
        right: margin,
        bottom: margin,
        left: margin,
        background: "#ffffff",
      })
      .flatten({ background: "#ffffff" })
      .png()
      .toBuffer();

    res
      .type("png")
      .set("Cache-Control", "public, max-age=31536000, immutable")
      .set("X-Branding-Version", settings.updatedAt.toISOString())
      .send(icon);
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof ObjectNotFoundError) {
      res.status(404).json({ error: "Logotypen finns inte." });
      return;
    }
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte skapa appikonen." });
  }
});

router.get("/manifest.webmanifest", async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    const settings = await getSettings();
    const branding = toResponse(settings);
    const version = encodeURIComponent(branding.updatedAt);
    res
      .type("application/manifest+json")
      .set("Cache-Control", "no-cache, max-age=0, must-revalidate")
      .json({
        name: `${branding.companyName} – Lagersystem`,
        short_name: branding.companyName,
        description: `Lagersystem för virke – ${branding.companyName}`,
        theme_color: "#2d5a27",
        background_color: "#ffffff",
        display: "standalone",
        start_url: "/",
        scope: "/",
        lang: "sv",
        icons: [
          {
            src: `/api/branding/icon?size=192&v=${version}`,
            sizes: "192x192",
            type: "image/png",
          },
          {
            src: `/api/branding/icon?size=512&v=${version}`,
            sizes: "512x512",
            type: "image/png",
            purpose: "any maskable",
          },
        ],
      });
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte skapa appmanifestet." });
  }
});

router.get("/", async (_req, res) => {
  try {
    res.json(toResponse(await getSettings()));
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte hämta företagsprofilen." });
  }
});

router.put("/", requireAdmin, async (req, res) => {
  const companyName = typeof req.body?.companyName === "string" ? req.body.companyName.trim() : "";
  const logoPath = req.body?.logoPath === null
    ? null
    : typeof req.body?.logoPath === "string"
      && (/^\/objects\/uploads\/[a-zA-Z0-9-]+$/.test(req.body.logoPath)
        || /^\/[a-zA-Z0-9][a-zA-Z0-9._/-]*$/.test(req.body.logoPath))
      ? req.body.logoPath
      : undefined;

  if (companyName.length < 1 || companyName.length > 120) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Företagsnamnet måste vara mellan 1 och 120 tecken." });
    return;
  }
  if (logoPath === undefined && req.body?.logoPath !== undefined) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltig logotypreferens." });
    return;
  }
  const optionalFields = [
    "streetAddress", "postalCode", "city", "country", "phone", "email", "organizationNumber",
    "vatNumber", "website", "certificationText", "certificationNumber", "invoiceDetails",
  ] as const;
  const profileValues: Record<string, string | boolean | null> = {};
  for (const field of optionalFields) {
    if (req.body?.[field] === undefined) continue;
    if (req.body[field] !== null && typeof req.body[field] !== "string") {
      res.status(400).json({ error: `Ogiltigt värde för ${field}.` });
      return;
    }
    const value = typeof req.body[field] === "string" ? req.body[field].trim() : null;
    if (["streetAddress", "postalCode", "city", "country", "phone", "email"].includes(field) && !value) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: `${field} får inte vara tomt.` });
      return;
    }
    if (value !== null && value.length > 500) {
      res.status(400).json({ error: `${field} är för långt.` });
      return;
    }
    profileValues[field] = value;
  }
  if (req.body?.approvedForFTax !== undefined) {
    if (typeof req.body.approvedForFTax !== "boolean") {
      res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltigt värde för approvedForFTax." });
      return;
    }
    profileValues.approvedForFTax = req.body.approvedForFTax;
  }

  try {
    const values = { companyName, ...profileValues, ...(logoPath === undefined ? {} : { logoPath }) };
    const [row] = await db
      .insert(brandingSettingsTable)
      .values({ id: 1, ...values })
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: brandingSettingsTable.id,
        set: { ...values, updatedAt: new Date() },
      })
      .returning();
    res.json(toResponse(row));
  } catch {
    res.status(500).json({ error: "Kunde inte spara företagsprofilen." });
  }
});

export default router;

<section>
  <h2>Autentisering</h2>
  <p>Alla extern-endpoints kräver en API-nyckel som skickas i HTTP-headern <code>X-API-Key</code>.</p>
