router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
  const params = ListLagerPosterQueryParams.parse(req.query);
  const { search, plats, varuslag, omradeId, fackId, status, page = 1, limit = 50 } = params;

  const conditions: SQL[] = [];

  if (search) {
    conditions.push(...buildLagerSearchConditions(search));
  }

  if (plats) {
    conditions.push(ilike(lagerPosterTable.plats, `%${plats}%`));
  }

  if (varuslag) {
    conditions.push(ilike(lagerPosterTable.varuslag, `%${varuslag}%`));
  }

  // Strukturerad filtrering på område/fack (via FK, inte fritext-plats).
  // fackId="none" betyder "poster i området som saknar fack" (ej angivet).
  if (omradeId != null) {
    conditions.push(eq(lagerPosterTable.omradeId, omradeId));
  }
  if (fackId != null) {
    if (fackId === "none") {
      conditions.push(isNull(lagerPosterTable.fackId));
    } else {
      const fackIdNum = Number(fackId);
      if (!isNaN(fackIdNum)) {
        conditions.push(eq(lagerPosterTable.fackId, fackIdNum));
      }
    }
  }

  // Exkludera alltid spec-importerade paket och mjukraderade paket från lagerlistan
  conditions.push(or(isNull(lagerPosterTable.avrappTyp), ne(lagerPosterTable.avrappTyp, 'spec_import'))!);
  conditions.push(isNull(lagerPosterTable.raderadAt));

  if (status === "i_lager") {
    // Samma regler som område-/fackstatistiken i routes/omraden.ts använder,
    // via den delade `iLagerExtraConditions()` (datumUt/impregnerare-delen —
    // spec_import/raderad_at ovan gäller redan alla statusar).
    conditions.push(...iLagerExtraConditions());
  } else if (status === "utlevererat") {
    conditions.push(isNotNull(lagerPosterTable.datumUt));
  }

  const where = conditions.length > 0 ? and(...conditions) : undefined;

  const offset = (page - 1) * limit;

  const orderByExpr = search
    ? sql`CASE WHEN ${lagerPosterTable.pktNr} ~ '^[0-9]+$' THEN LPAD(${lagerPosterTable.pktNr}, 20, '0') ELSE ${lagerPosterTable.pktNr} END, ${lagerPosterTable.id}`
    : sql`${lagerPosterTable.id}`;

  const [rows, countResult] = await Promise.all([
    db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(where)
      .orderBy(orderByExpr)
      .limit(limit)
      .offset(offset),
    db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(where),
  ]);

  const total = Number(countResult[0]?.count ?? 0);

  // Hämta spec-info (utkast) för de hämtade raderna
  const rowIds = rows.map(r => r.id);
  const specInfoMap = new Map<number, { specId: number; kund: string | null; status: string }[]>();
  if (rowIds.length > 0) {
    const specRows = await db
      .select({
        lagerPostId: specPaketTable.lagerPostId,
        specId: specPaketTable.specId,
        kund: specifikationerTable.kund,
        status: specifikationerTable.status,
      })
      .from(specPaketTable)
      .innerJoin(specifikationerTable, eq(specPaketTable.specId, specifikationerTable.id))
      .where(inArray(specPaketTable.lagerPostId, rowIds));
    for (const s of specRows) {
      const arr = specInfoMap.get(s.lagerPostId) ?? [];
      arr.push({ specId: s.specId, kund: s.kund, status: s.status });
      specInfoMap.set(s.lagerPostId, arr);
    }
  }

  const mappedRows = await mapRowsWithStructuredPlace(rows);
  res.json({
    data: mappedRows.map(r => ({ ...r, specs: specInfoMap.get(r.id) ?? [] })),
    total,
    page,
    limit,
    totalPages: Math.ceil(total / limit),
  });
});

// Ändra plats-dialogen har två lägen: fritext (skriver bara `plats`) och
// strukturerad område+fack (t.ex. Röda Magasin → V01). Denna funktion
// tolkar och validerar den strukturerade delen av en batch-patch —
// serverseidan litar aldrig på klientens omradeId/fackId-kombination utan
// kontrollerar den mot databasen (facket måste faktiskt tillhöra angivet
// område, båda måste vara aktiva).
//
// Kontraktet från klienten:
//  - `omradeId` satt till ett tal  => strukturerad placering. `plats`
//    härleds alltid från områdets namn på servern (klientens ev. skickade
//    `plats` ignoreras) så de två fälten aldrig kan hamna i otakt.
//    `fackId` (tal) väljer ett specifikt fack; `fackId` null/utelämnad
//    betyder "Ej angivet fack".
//  - `omradeId` satt till null      => explicit rensning av strukturerad
//    placering (används inte av UI idag, men stöds för symmetri).
//  - `omradeId` inte alls med i patchen, men `plats` är det => klassisk
//    fritextflytt. Eftersom "Ändra plats" är den enda flyttvägen i appen
//    innebär varje sådant anrop en fullständig flytt till en ny plats, så
//    ev. gammal strukturerad område/fack-koppling rensas (kravet att inga
//    inaktuella kopplingar ska bli kvar).
// Extraherar transaktionstypen direkt från db.transaction så att hjälpfunktioner
// nedan kan ta emot `tx` utan att duplicera drizzle/pg-typerna manuellt.
export type LagerTx = Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0];

// Verksamhetsregel: ett pktNr är ett fysiskt paket/paketlapp, även om det
// ligger som flera lager_poster-rader (en per längd). Platsändring och
// utleverans (Ändra plats / Skanna ut) ska därför alltid behandla samtliga
// AKTUELLA (iLagerConditions) rader med samma pktNr som en enhet, aldrig
// bara den/de rader som råkade vara markerade. Ursprungligen valda id:n tas
// alltid med, även om de undantagsvis inte skulle uppfylla iLagerConditions
// (t.ex. redan utlevererad rad som ändå explicit valdes) — vi tar aldrig
// bort en rad användaren uttryckligen valde.
export async function expandIdsTillKompletaPaket(tx: LagerTx, ids: number[]): Promise<number[]> {
  if (ids.length === 0) return ids;

  const valda = await tx
    .select({ id: lagerPosterTable.id, pktNr: lagerPosterTable.pktNr })
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.id, ids));

  const pktNrLista = Array.from(
    new Set(valda.map(r => r.pktNr?.trim()).filter((p): p is string => !!p)),
  );

  const resultat = new Set<number>(ids);
  if (pktNrLista.length > 0) {
    const paketRader = await tx
      .select({ id: lagerPosterTable.id })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(and(inArray(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNrLista), ...iLagerConditions()));
    for (const r of paketRader) resultat.add(r.id);
  }
  return Array.from(resultat);
}

// Delad kärna för "uppdatera placering/status för ett eller flera paket,
// atomiskt per pktNr". Används av både PUT /batch (Ändra plats / Skanna ut)
// och inventeringens /korrigera-endpoint (routes/inventering.ts), så båda
// vägarna alltid expanderar till kompletta paket och loggar historik på
// exakt samma sätt — bara `action` skiljer dem åt i historiken.
export async function applyLagerPatch(
  ids: number[],
  patch: Record<string, unknown>,
  opts: { userId?: string | null; action: string },
): Promise<(typeof lagerPosterTable.$inferSelect)[]> {
  const paverkarPlatsEllerUtleverans =
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(patch, "omradeId") ||
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(patch, "fackId") ||
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(patch, "plats") ||
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(patch, "datumUt");

  const updated = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
    const finalIds = paverkarPlatsEllerUtleverans
      ? await expandIdsTillKompletaPaket(tx, ids)
      : Array.from(new Set(ids));

    return tx
      .update(lagerPosterTable)
      .set({ ...patch, uppdateradAt: new Date() })
      .where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.id, finalIds))
      .returning();
  });

  for (const row of updated) {
    await logHistorik(row.id, opts.action, opts.userId, patch);
  }

  return updated;
}

router.put("/batch", async (req, res): Promise<void> => {
  const parsed = UpdateLagerBatchBody.safeParse(req.body);
  if (!parsed.success) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: "Ogiltig batchuppdatering" });
    return;
  }
  const { ids, patch: rawPatch } = parsed.data;

  const resolved = await resolveStructuredPlatsPatch(rawPatch);
  if (!resolved.ok) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: resolved.error });
    return;
  }

  const updated = await applyLagerPatch(ids, resolved.patch, { userId: req.userId, action: "batch_update" });

  res.json({ uppdaterade: updated.length });
});

router.delete("/batch", requireAdmin, async (req, res) => {
  const { ids } = req.body as { ids: number[] };
  if (!Array.isArray(ids) || ids.length === 0) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "ids saknas" });
  }

  const now = new Date();
  for (const id of ids) {
    await logHistorik(id, "soft_delete", req.userId);
  }

  await db
    .update(lagerPosterTable)
    .set({ raderadAt: now, uppdateradAt: now })
    .where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.id, ids));
  res.json({ raderade: ids.length });
});

router.get("/historik", async (req, res) => {
  const { from, to, action, page = "1", limit = "50" } = req.query as Record<string, string>;
  const pageNum = Math.max(1, parseInt(page));
  const limitNum = Math.min(200, Math.max(1, parseInt(limit)));
  const offset = (pageNum - 1) * limitNum;

  const conditions = [];
  if (from) conditions.push(gte(lagerHistorikTable.skapadAt, new Date(from)));
  if (to) {
    const toDate = new Date(to);
    toDate.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
    conditions.push(lte(lagerHistorikTable.skapadAt, toDate));
  }
  if (action) conditions.push(eq(lagerHistorikTable.action, action));

  const where = conditions.length > 0 ? and(...conditions) : undefined;

  const [rows, countResult] = await Promise.all([
    db.select({
      id: lagerHistorikTable.id,
      lagerId: lagerHistorikTable.lagerId,
      action: lagerHistorikTable.action,
      andringar: lagerHistorikTable.andringar,
      changedBy: lagerHistorikTable.changedBy,
      skapadAt: lagerHistorikTable.skapadAt,
      pktNr: lagerPosterTable.pktNr,
      varuslag: lagerPosterTable.varuslag,
      profil: lagerPosterTable.profil,
    })
    .from(lagerHistorikTable)
    .leftJoin(lagerPosterTable, eq(lagerHistorikTable.lagerId, lagerPosterTable.id))
    .where(where)
    .orderBy(desc(lagerHistorikTable.skapadAt))
    .limit(limitNum)
    .offset(offset),
    db.select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)::int` }).from(lagerHistorikTable).where(where),
  ]);

  res.json({
    poster: rows.map(r => ({
      ...r,
      skapadAt: r.skapadAt.toISOString(),
      andringar: r.andringar ? (() => { try { return JSON.parse(r.andringar!); } catch { return null; } })() : null,
    })),
    total: countResult[0]?.count ?? 0,
    page: pageNum,
    limit: limitNum,
  });
});

router.get("/:id/historik", async (req, res) => {
  const id = Number(req.params.id);
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(lagerHistorikTable)
    .where(eq(lagerHistorikTable.lagerId, id))
    .orderBy(lagerHistorikTable.skapadAt);
  res.json(rows.map(r => ({
    ...r,
    skapadAt: r.skapadAt.toISOString(),
    andringar: r.andringar ? JSON.parse(r.andringar) : null,
  })));
});

router.get("/by-pktnr/:pktNr", async (req, res) => {
  const pktNr = String(req.params.pktNr).trim();
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(and(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNr), isNull(lagerPosterTable.raderadAt)))
    .limit(1);
  if (!rows[0]) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: `Paket med PKT-NR "${pktNr}" hittades inte` });
    return;
  }
  const [mapped] = await mapRowsWithStructuredPlace([rows[0]]);
  res.json(mapped);
});

router.get("/alla-pktnr/:pktNr", async (req, res) => {
  const pktNr = String(req.params.pktNr).trim();
  const rows = await db
    .select()
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(and(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNr), isNull(lagerPosterTable.raderadAt)));
  res.json(await mapRowsWithStructuredPlace(rows));
});

// Paketvy: ett pktNr är ett fysiskt paket, men kan ligga som flera
// lager_poster-rader (en per längd). Denna route returnerar samtliga
// AKTUELLA rader (iLagerConditions) för paketet i ett svar, så att t.ex.
// Skanna ut kan behandla paketet atomiskt istället för att bara hämta en
// godtycklig rad. 404 med samma felformat som /by-pktnr om inga aktuella
// rader finns, så befintlig felhantering i klienten (inkl. fallback som
// stryper inledande bokstäver) fungerar oförändrat.
router.get("/paket/:pktNr", async (req, res) => {
  const pktNr = String(req.params.pktNr).trim();
  const rows = await hamtaAktuellaPaketRader(pktNr);

  if (rows.length === 0) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: `Paket med PKT-NR "${pktNr}" hittades inte` });
    return;
  }

  const mapped = await mapRowsWithStructuredPlace(rows);
  res.json({
    pktNr,
    rader: mapped,
    antalRader: mapped.length,
    totalAntal: mapped.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (r.antal ?? 0), 0),
    totalM3: mapped.reduce((sum, r) => sum + (r.m3 ?? 0), 0),
  });
});

router.get("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = GetLagerPostParams.parse({ id: Number(req.params.id) });
  const row = await db
    .select()
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, id))
    .limit(1);

  if (!row[0]) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Hittades inte" });
    return;
  }

  const [mapped] = await mapRowsWithStructuredPlace([row[0]]);
  res.json(mapped);
});

router.post("/check-duplicates", async (req, res) => {
  const { pktNrs } = req.body as { pktNrs: string[] };
  if (!Array.isArray(pktNrs) || pktNrs.length === 0) { res.json({ existing: [] }); return; }
  const rows = await db
    .select({ pktNr: lagerPosterTable.pktNr, id: lagerPosterTable.id, datumUt: lagerPosterTable.datumUt })
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, pktNrs));
  res.json({
    existing: rows.map(r => r.pktNr),
    avrapporterade: rows.filter(r => r.datumUt != null).map(r => r.pktNr),
  });
});

router.post("/upsert", async (req, res) => {
  const parsedBody = CreateLagerPostBody.parse(req.body);
  const normalizedBody = {
    ...parsedBody,
    ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsedBody, "behandling")
      ? { behandling: normaliseraPaketBehandling(parsedBody.behandling) }
      : {}),
    ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsedBody, "utforande")
      ? { utforande: normaliseraPaketUtforande(parsedBody.utforande) }
      : {}),
  };
  const structuredResult = await resolveStructuredPlatsPatch(normalizedBody);
  if (!structuredResult.ok) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: structuredResult.error });
    return;
  }
  const body = structuredResult.patch as typeof normalizedBody;

  if (body.pktNr) {
    const existing = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.pktNr, body.pktNr))
      .limit(1);

    if (existing[0]) {
      const existingRow = existing[0];
      const isAvrapporterad = existingRow.datumUt != null;
      const updated = await db
        .update(lagerPosterTable)
        .set({
          ...body,
          nomTjocklek: body.nomTjocklek?.toString(),
          nomBredd: body.nomBredd?.toString(),
          actTjocklek: body.actTjocklek?.toString(),
          actBredd: body.actBredd?.toString(),
          langdDm: body.langdDm?.toString(),
          m3: body.m3?.toString(),
          m3Pris: body.m3Pris?.toString(),
          m3Sum: body.m3Sum?.toString(),
          avrappM3: isAvrapporterad ? existingRow.avrappM3 : body.avrappM3?.toString(),
          lpm: body.lpm?.toString(),
          actM3: body.actM3?.toString(),
          datumUt: isAvrapporterad ? existingRow.datumUt : (body.datumUt ?? null),
          avrapp: isAvrapporterad ? existingRow.avrapp : (body.avrapp ?? null),
          uppdateradAt: new Date(),
        })
        .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, existingRow.id))
        .returning();
      res.json({ ...mapRow(updated[0]), _action: "updated" });
      return;
    }
  }

  const inserted = await db
    .insert(lagerPosterTable)
    .values({
      ...body,
      nomTjocklek: body.nomTjocklek?.toString(),
      nomBredd: body.nomBredd?.toString(),
      actTjocklek: body.actTjocklek?.toString(),
      actBredd: body.actBredd?.toString(),
      langdDm: body.langdDm?.toString(),
      m3: body.m3?.toString(),
      m3Pris: body.m3Pris?.toString(),
      m3Sum: body.m3Sum?.toString(),
      avrappM3: body.avrappM3?.toString(),
      lpm: body.lpm?.toString(),
      actM3: body.actM3?.toString(),
    })
    .returning();

  res.status(201).json({ ...mapRow(inserted[0]), _action: "created" });
});

router.post("/", async (req, res) => {
  const parsedBody = CreateLagerPostBody.parse(req.body);
  const body = {
    ...parsedBody,
    ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsedBody, "behandling")
      ? { behandling: normaliseraPaketBehandling(parsedBody.behandling) }
      : {}),
    ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsedBody, "utforande")
      ? { utforande: normaliseraPaketUtforande(parsedBody.utforande) }
      : {}),
  };

  const values = lagerInsertValues(body);
  try {
    const inserted = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
      await lasPaketnummerOchKontrolleraLedigt(tx, values.pktNr);
      const [row] = await tx
        .insert(lagerPosterTable)
        .values(values)
        .returning();
      await tx.insert(lagerHistorikTable).values({
        lagerId: row.id,
        action: "create",
        changedBy: (req as unknown as { userId?: string }).userId ?? null,
      });
      return row;
    });
    res.status(201).json(mapRow(inserted));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof PaketnummerConflictError) {
      res.status(409).json({ error: error.message });
      return;
    }
    throw error;
  }
});

router.post("/manual-batch", async (req, res) => {
  const { poster } = CreateManualLagerBatchBody.parse(req.body);
  const normalized = poster.map((post) =>
    lagerInsertValues(
      CreateLagerPostBody.parse({
        ...post,
        ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(post, "behandling")
          ? { behandling: normaliseraPaketBehandling(post.behandling) }
          : {}),
        ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(post, "utforande")
          ? { utforande: normaliseraPaketUtforande(post.utforande) }
          : {}),
      }),
    ),
  );
  const pktNrs = [
    ...new Set(normalized.flatMap((post) => post.pktNr ? [post.pktNr] : [])),
  ];
  if (pktNrs.length > 1) {
    res.status(400).json({
      error: "Alla längdrader i en manuell batch måste ha samma paketnummer.",
    });
    return;
  }
  const pktNr = pktNrs[0] ?? null;

  try {
    const inserted = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
      await lasPaketnummerOchKontrolleraLedigt(tx, pktNr);
      const rows = await tx
        .insert(lagerPosterTable)
        .values(normalized)
        .returning();
      if (rows.length > 0) {
        await tx.insert(lagerHistorikTable).values(
          rows.map((row) => ({
            lagerId: row.id,
            action: "create",
            changedBy: (req as unknown as { userId?: string }).userId ?? null,
          })),
        );
      }
      return rows;
    });
    res.status(201).json(inserted.map(mapRow));
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof PaketnummerConflictError) {
      res.status(409).json({ error: error.message });
      return;
    }
    throw error;
  }
});

router.put("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = UpdateLagerPostParams.parse({ id: Number(req.params.id) });
  const parsedBody = UpdateLagerPostBody.parse(req.body);
  const body = {
    ...parsedBody,
    ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsedBody, "behandling")
      ? { behandling: normaliseraPaketBehandling(parsedBody.behandling) }
      : {}),
    ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsedBody, "pktNr")
      ? { pktNr: normaliseraPaketnummer(parsedBody.pktNr) }
      : {}),
    ...(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(parsedBody, "utforande")
      ? { utforande: normaliseraPaketUtforande(parsedBody.utforande) }
      : {}),
  };
  const locationFields = ["plats", "omradeId", "fackId"] as const;
  const changesLocation = locationFields.some((field) =>
    Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(body, field),
  );

  const resolved = changesLocation
    ? await resolveStructuredPlatsPatch(body as Record<string, unknown>)
    : { ok: true as const, patch: body as Record<string, unknown> };
  if (!resolved.ok) {
    res.status(400).json({ error: resolved.error });
    return;
  }

  const resolvedBody = resolved.patch;
  const locationPatch = changesLocation
    ? Object.fromEntries(locationFields.map((field) => [field, resolvedBody[field]]))
    : {};
  const scalarBody = Object.fromEntries(
    Object.entries(resolvedBody).filter(([field]) => !locationFields.includes(field as typeof locationFields[number])),
  );

  const toDbUpdate = (data: Record<string, unknown>) => ({
    ...data,
    nomTjocklek: data.nomTjocklek !== undefined ? data.nomTjocklek == null ? null : String(data.nomTjocklek) : undefined,
    nomBredd: data.nomBredd !== undefined ? data.nomBredd == null ? null : String(data.nomBredd) : undefined,
    actTjocklek: data.actTjocklek !== undefined ? data.actTjocklek == null ? null : String(data.actTjocklek) : undefined,
    actBredd: data.actBredd !== undefined ? data.actBredd == null ? null : String(data.actBredd) : undefined,
    langdDm: data.langdDm !== undefined ? data.langdDm == null ? null : String(data.langdDm) : undefined,
    m3: data.m3 !== undefined ? data.m3 == null ? null : String(data.m3) : undefined,
    m3Pris: data.m3Pris !== undefined ? data.m3Pris == null ? null : String(data.m3Pris) : undefined,
    m3Sum: data.m3Sum !== undefined ? data.m3Sum == null ? null : String(data.m3Sum) : undefined,
    avrappM3: data.avrappM3 !== undefined ? data.avrappM3 == null ? null : String(data.avrappM3) : undefined,
    lpm: data.lpm !== undefined ? data.lpm == null ? null : String(data.lpm) : undefined,
    actM3: data.actM3 !== undefined ? data.actM3 == null ? null : String(data.actM3) : undefined,
  });

  let transactionResult;
  try {
    transactionResult = await db.transaction(async (tx) => {
      const [current] = await tx
        .select({ pktNr: lagerPosterTable.pktNr })
        .from(lagerPosterTable)
        .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, id))
        .limit(1);
      const requestedPktNr = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(scalarBody, "pktNr")
        ? normaliseraPaketnummer(scalarBody.pktNr as string | null | undefined)
        : undefined;
      if (
        current &&
        requestedPktNr &&
        !sammaPaketnummer(current.pktNr, requestedPktNr)
      ) {
        await lasPaketnummerOchKontrolleraLedigt(tx, requestedPktNr);
      }

      const ids = changesLocation ? await expandIdsTillKompletaPaket(tx, [id]) : [id];
      if (changesLocation && ids.length > 0) {
        await tx
          .update(lagerPosterTable)
          .set({ ...locationPatch, uppdateradAt: new Date() })
          .where(inArray(lagerPosterTable.id, ids));
      }

      const updatedSelected = await tx
        .update(lagerPosterTable)
        .set({ ...toDbUpdate(scalarBody), ...(changesLocation ? locationPatch : {}), uppdateradAt: new Date() })
        .where(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, id))
        .returning();

      return { ids, selected: updatedSelected[0] };
    });
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof PaketnummerConflictError) {
      res.status(409).json({ error: error.message });
      return;
    }
    throw error;
  }

  if (!transactionResult.selected) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Hittades inte" });
    return;
  }

  for (const rowId of transactionResult.ids) {
    await logHistorik(
      rowId,
      "update",
      req.userId,
      rowId === id ? resolvedBody : locationPatch,
    );
  }
  const [mapped] = await mapRowsWithStructuredPlace([transactionResult.selected]);
  res.json(mapped);
});

router.delete("/:id", requireAdmin, async (req, res) => {
  const { id } = DeleteLagerPostParams.parse({ id: Number(req.params.id) });
  const now = new Date();

  const updated = await db
    .update(lagerPosterTable)
    .set({ raderadAt: now, uppdateradAt: now })
    .where(and(eq(lagerPosterTable.id, id), isNull(lagerPosterTable.raderadAt)))
    .returning();

  if (!updated[0]) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Hittades inte" });
    return;
  }

  await logHistorik(id, "soft_delete", req.userId);
  res.status(204).send();
});

router.post("/import", async (req, res) => {
  const body = ImportLagerDataBody.parse(req.body);
  let importerade = 0;
  let fel = 0;

  for (const post of body.poster) {
    try {
      await db.insert(lagerPosterTable).values({
        ...post,
        nomTjocklek: post.nomTjocklek?.toString(),
        nomBredd: post.nomBredd?.toString(),
        actTjocklek: post.actTjocklek?.toString(),
        actBredd: post.actBredd?.toString(),
        langdDm: post.langdDm?.toString(),
        m3: post.m3?.toString(),
        m3Pris: post.m3Pris?.toString(),
        m3Sum: post.m3Sum?.toString(),
        avrappM3: post.avrappM3?.toString(),
        lpm: post.lpm?.toString(),
        actM3: post.actM3?.toString(),
      });
      importerade++;
    } catch {
      fel++;
    }
  }

  res.json({ importerade, fel });
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/lagerplatser.ts ================

import { Router } from "express";
import { db } from "@workspace/db";
import { lagerplatsConfigTable, lagerPosterTable } from "@workspace/db/schema";
import { eq, isNotNull } from "drizzle-orm";

const router = Router();

const CANVAS_W = 1000;
const CANVAS_H = 1163;
const DEFAULT_W = 170;
const DEFAULT_H = 90;
const COLS = 4;
const GAP = 20;
const PAD_X = 30;
const PAD_Y = 30;

type ZoneInput = { namn: string; x: number; y: number; bredd: number; hojd: number; farg?: string | null };

function autoPositions(names: string[]) {
  return names.map((namn, i) => {
    const col = i % COLS;
    const row = Math.floor(i / COLS);
    return {
      namn,
      x: PAD_X + col * (DEFAULT_W + GAP),
      y: PAD_Y + row * (DEFAULT_H + GAP),
      bredd: DEFAULT_W,
      hojd: DEFAULT_H,
      farg: null as string | null,
    };
  });
}

router.get("/config", async (_req, res) => {
  const [configs, platserRows] = await Promise.all([
    db.select().from(lagerplatsConfigTable),
    db.selectDistinct({ plats: lagerPosterTable.plats })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(isNotNull(lagerPosterTable.plats)),
  ]);

  const allNames = platserRows.map(r => r.plats!).filter(Boolean).sort();

  if (configs.length === 0) {
    if (allNames.length > 0) {
      const positions = autoPositions(allNames);
      const inserted = await db.insert(lagerplatsConfigTable).values(positions).returning();
      res.json(inserted);
    } else {
      res.json([]);
    }
    return;
  }

  const existingNames = new Set(configs.map(c => c.namn));
  const newNames = allNames.filter(n => !existingNames.has(n));
  if (newNames.length > 0) {
    const newPositions = autoPositions(newNames);
    const inserted = await db.insert(lagerplatsConfigTable).values(newPositions).returning();
    res.json([...configs, ...inserted]);
    return;
  }

  res.json(configs);
});

router.put("/config", async (req, res) => {
  const zones: ZoneInput[] = req.body;
  const result: typeof lagerplatsConfigTable.$inferSelect[] = [];
  for (const zone of zones) {
    const existing = await db
      .select()
      .from(lagerplatsConfigTable)
      .where(eq(lagerplatsConfigTable.namn, zone.namn))
      .limit(1);

    if (existing[0]) {
      const [updated] = await db
        .update(lagerplatsConfigTable)
        .set({ x: zone.x, y: zone.y, bredd: zone.bredd, hojd: zone.hojd, farg: zone.farg ?? null })
        .where(eq(lagerplatsConfigTable.id, existing[0].id))
        .returning();
      result.push(updated);
    } else {
      const [inserted] = await db
        .insert(lagerplatsConfigTable)
        .values({ namn: zone.namn, x: zone.x, y: zone.y, bredd: zone.bredd, hojd: zone.hojd, farg: zone.farg ?? null })
        .returning();
      result.push(inserted);
    }
  }
  res.json(result);
});

router.post("/config/auto", async (_req, res) => {
  const platserRows = await db
    .selectDistinct({ plats: lagerPosterTable.plats })
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(isNotNull(lagerPosterTable.plats));
  const names = platserRows.map(r => r.plats!).filter(Boolean).sort();
  await db.delete(lagerplatsConfigTable);
  if (names.length === 0) { res.json([]); return; }
  const positions = autoPositions(names);
  const inserted = await db.insert(lagerplatsConfigTable).values(positions).returning();
  res.json(inserted);
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/larm.ts ================

import { Router } from "express";
import { db, lagerLarmTable, lagerPosterTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { eq, isNull, ilike, and, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { requireAdmin } from "../app";

const router = Router();

function mapLarm(r: typeof lagerLarmTable.$inferSelect) {
  return {
    ...r,
    minM3: r.minM3 ? Number(r.minM3) : 0,
    nomTjocklek: r.nomTjocklek ? Number(r.nomTjocklek) : null,
    nomBredd: r.nomBredd ? Number(r.nomBredd) : null,
    langdDm: r.langdDm ? Number(r.langdDm) : null,
  };
}

router.get("/", async (_req, res) => {
  const rows = await db.select().from(lagerLarmTable).orderBy(lagerLarmTable.skapadAt);
  res.json(rows.map(mapLarm));
});

router.get("/status", async (_req, res) => {
  const larm = await db.select().from(lagerLarmTable).where(eq(lagerLarmTable.aktiv, true));
  const triggered = [];

  for (const l of larm) {
    const conditions = [isNull(lagerPosterTable.datumUt), isNull(lagerPosterTable.raderadAt)];
    if (l.nomTjocklek) {
      conditions.push(sql`${lagerPosterTable.nomTjocklek}::numeric = ${Number(l.nomTjocklek)}`);
    }
    if (l.nomBredd) {
      conditions.push(sql`${lagerPosterTable.nomBredd}::numeric = ${Number(l.nomBredd)}`);
    }
    if (l.langdDm) {
      conditions.push(sql`${lagerPosterTable.langdDm}::numeric = ${Number(l.langdDm)}`);
    }
    if (l.varuslag) {
      conditions.push(ilike(lagerPosterTable.traslag, l.varuslag));
    }
    if (l.profil) {
      conditions.push(ilike(lagerPosterTable.profil, l.profil));
    }

    const result = await db
      .select({ totalM3: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${lagerPosterTable.m3}::numeric), 0)` })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(and(...conditions));

    const currentM3 = Number(result[0]?.totalM3 ?? 0);
    const minM3 = Number(l.minM3);

    if (currentM3 < minM3) {
      triggered.push({
        id: l.id,
        namn: l.namn,
        currentM3: Math.round(currentM3 * 1000) / 1000,
        minM3,
        nomTjocklek: l.nomTjocklek ? Number(l.nomTjocklek) : null,
        nomBredd: l.nomBredd ? Number(l.nomBredd) : null,
        langdDm: l.langdDm ? Number(l.langdDm) : null,
        varuslag: l.varuslag,
        profil: l.profil,
      });
    }
  }

  res.json(triggered);
});

router.post("/", requireAdmin, async (req, res) => {
  const { namn, nomTjocklek, nomBredd, langdDm, varuslag, profil, minM3 } = req.body;
  if (!namn || minM3 === undefined) {
    return res.status(400).json({ error: "namn och minM3 krävs" });
  }

  const inserted = await db
    .insert(lagerLarmTable)
    .values({
      namn,
      nomTjocklek: nomTjocklek != null ? String(nomTjocklek) : null,
      nomBredd: nomBredd != null ? String(nomBredd) : null,
      langdDm: langdDm != null ? String(langdDm) : null,
      varuslag: varuslag ?? null,
      profil: profil ?? null,
      minM3: String(minM3),
      aktiv: true,
    })
    .returning();

  res.status(201).json(mapLarm(inserted[0]));
});

router.put("/:id", requireAdmin, async (req, res) => {
  const id = Number(req.params.id);
  const { namn, nomTjocklek, nomBredd, langdDm, varuslag, profil, minM3, aktiv } = req.body;

  const patch: Record<string, unknown> = {};
  if (namn !== undefined) patch.namn = namn;
  if (nomTjocklek !== undefined) patch.nomTjocklek = nomTjocklek != null ? String(nomTjocklek) : null;
  if (nomBredd !== undefined) patch.nomBredd = nomBredd != null ? String(nomBredd) : null;
  if (langdDm !== undefined) patch.langdDm = langdDm != null ? String(langdDm) : null;
  if (varuslag !== undefined) patch.varuslag = varuslag;
  if (profil !== undefined) patch.profil = profil;
  if (minM3 !== undefined) patch.minM3 = String(minM3);
  if (aktiv !== undefined) patch.aktiv = aktiv;

  const updated = await db
    .update(lagerLarmTable)
    .set(patch)
    .where(eq(lagerLarmTable.id, id))
    .returning();

  if (!updated[0]) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Hittades inte" });
  res.json(mapLarm(updated[0]));
});

router.delete("/:id", requireAdmin, async (req, res) => {
  const id = Number(req.params.id);
  await db.delete(lagerLarmTable).where(eq(lagerLarmTable.id, id));
  res.status(204).send();
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/lego.ts ================

import { Router } from "express";
import { db, legoJobbTable, legoPaketTable, specifikationerTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { lagerPosterTable, brandingSettingsTable } from "@workspace/db/schema";
import { eq, desc, sql, isNotNull, and, isNull } from "drizzle-orm";
import XLSX from "xlsx";
import { brandingToDocumentProfile, documentSenderSource, hasSenderSnapshot, missingDocumentProfileFields, profileResponse, senderSnapshotForCapture, senderSnapshotProfile, type DocumentProfile } from "../lib/documentProfile";

const router = Router();

function toNum(val: unknown): number | null {
  if (val === null || val === undefined || val === "") return null;
  const n = Number(val);
  return isNaN(n) ? null : n;
}

function mapPaket(p: typeof legoPaketTable.$inferSelect) {
  return {
    ...p,
    rawTjocklek: toNum(p.rawTjocklek),
    rawBredd: toNum(p.rawBredd),
    hyvlTjocklek: toNum(p.hyvlTjocklek),
    hyvlBredd: toNum(p.hyvlBredd),
    langdDm: toNum(p.langdDm),
    m3: toNum(p.m3),
    lpm: toNum(p.lpm),
    prisM3: toNum(p.prisM3),
    skapadAt: p.skapadAt.toISOString(),
  };
}

function n(v: number | null, dec = 3) {
  return v != null ? parseFloat(v.toFixed(dec)) : null;
}

const profileFields = [
  "companyName", "logoPath", "streetAddress", "postalCode", "city", "country", "phone", "email",
  "organizationNumber", "vatNumber", "website", "certificationText", "certificationNumber", "invoiceDetails",
] as const;
type ProfileField = typeof profileFields[number];
const requiredAlternateFields = new Set<ProfileField>(["companyName", "streetAddress", "postalCode", "city", "country", "phone", "email"]);

function alternateSenderPatch(body: unknown): { values?: Record<string, string | boolean | null>; error?: string } {
  if (body === undefined) return {};
  if (body === null) return { values: { ...Object.fromEntries(profileFields.map((field) => [`alternateSender${field[0]!.toUpperCase()}${field.slice(1)}`, null])), alternateSenderApprovedForFTax: false } };
  if (typeof body !== "object" || Array.isArray(body)) return { error: "Ogiltig alternativ avsändare." };
  const input = body as Record<string, unknown>;
  const profile = {} as Record<ProfileField, string | null>;
  for (const field of profileFields) {
    const value = input[field];
    if (value !== undefined && value !== null && typeof value !== "string") return { error: `Ogiltigt värde för alternativ avsändare: ${field}.` };
    profile[field] = typeof value === "string" ? value.trim() : null;
    if (profile[field] && profile[field]!.length > 500) return { error: `Alternativ avsändare: ${field} är för långt.` };
  }
  if (profile.companyName && [...requiredAlternateFields].some((field) => !profile[field])) {
    return { error: "Alternativ avsändare måste innehålla fullständiga kontaktuppgifter." };
  }
  return { values: {
    ...Object.fromEntries(profileFields.map((field) => [`alternateSender${field[0]!.toUpperCase()}${field.slice(1)}`, profile[field]])),
    alternateSenderApprovedForFTax: input.approvedForFTax === true,
  } };
}

function alternateSenderProfile(row: typeof legoJobbTable.$inferSelect): DocumentProfile | null {
  if (!row.alternateSenderCompanyName) return null;
  return { ...Object.fromEntries(profileFields.map((field) => {
    const key = `alternateSender${field[0]!.toUpperCase()}${field.slice(1)}` as keyof typeof row;
    return [field, row[key]];
  })), approvedForFTax: row.alternateSenderApprovedForFTax } as DocumentProfile;
}

async function mapJobWithSender<T extends typeof legoJobbTable.$inferSelect>(job: T) {
  const [branding] = await db.select().from(brandingSettingsTable).where(eq(brandingSettingsTable.id, 1)).limit(1);
  if (!branding) {
    const alternateSender = alternateSenderProfile(job);
    return {
      ...job,
      sender: null,
      documentSenderSnapshot: null,
      useAlternateDocumentSender: alternateSender !== null,
      alternateSender,
    };
  }
  const documentSenderSnapshot = profileResponse(senderSnapshotProfile(job, brandingToDocumentProfile(branding)));
  const alternateSender = alternateSenderProfile(job);
  return {
    ...job,
    showCertificationOnDocument: hasSenderSnapshot(job)
      ? job.senderShowCertificationOnDocument
      : job.showCertificationOnDocument,
    sender: documentSenderSnapshot,
    documentSenderSnapshot,
    useAlternateDocumentSender: alternateSender !== null,
    alternateSender: alternateSender ? profileResponse(alternateSender) : null,
  };
}

async function captureLegoSenderSnapshot(job: typeof legoJobbTable.$inferSelect) {
  const [branding] = await db.select().from(brandingSettingsTable).where(eq(brandingSettingsTable.id, 1)).limit(1);
  if (!branding) return;
  const baseValues = senderSnapshotForCapture(job, documentSenderSource(alternateSenderProfile(job), brandingToDocumentProfile(branding)));
  const values = baseValues && { ...baseValues, senderShowCertificationOnDocument: job.showCertificationOnDocument };
  if (!values) return;
  await db.update(legoJobbTable).set(values)
    .where(and(eq(legoJobbTable.id, job.id), isNull(legoJobbTable.senderCompanyName)));
}

// GET /api/lego – list all jobs
router.get("/", async (req, res) => {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      jobb: legoJobbTable,
      jobbNr: legoJobbTable.jobbNr,
      kund: legoJobbTable.kund,
      datumIn: legoJobbTable.datumIn,
      datumUt: legoJobbTable.datumUt,
      status: legoJobbTable.status,
      anteckning: legoJobbTable.anteckning,
      kopplatJobbId: legoJobbTable.kopplatJobbId,
      kopplatSpecId: legoJobbTable.kopplatSpecId,
      skapadAt: legoJobbTable.skapadAt,
      antalPaket: sql<number>`COUNT(${legoPaketTable.id})::int`,
      totalM3: sql<number>`ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(${legoPaketTable.m3}::numeric), 0), 3)::float`,
      totalActM3: sql<number>`ROUND(COALESCE(SUM(
        CASE WHEN ${legoPaketTable.hyvlTjocklek} IS NOT NULL AND ${legoPaketTable.hyvlBredd} IS NOT NULL
          AND ${legoPaketTable.langdDm} IS NOT NULL AND ${legoPaketTable.antal} IS NOT NULL
          THEN (${legoPaketTable.hyvlTjocklek}::numeric / 1000)
             * (${legoPaketTable.hyvlBredd}::numeric / 1000)
             * (${legoPaketTable.langdDm}::numeric / 10)
             * ${legoPaketTable.antal}::numeric
          ELSE NULL END
      ), 0), 3)::float`,
      totalPris: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${legoPaketTable.m3}::numeric * ${legoPaketTable.prisM3}::numeric), 0)::float`,
    })
    .from(legoJobbTable)
    .leftJoin(legoPaketTable, eq(legoPaketTable.jobbId, legoJobbTable.id))
    .groupBy(legoJobbTable.id)
    .orderBy(desc(legoJobbTable.skapadAt));
  res.json(await Promise.all(rows.map(async (r) => {
    const mapped = await mapJobWithSender(r.jobb);
    return { ...mapped, antalPaket: r.antalPaket, totalM3: r.totalM3, totalActM3: r.totalActM3, totalPris: r.totalPris, skapadAt: r.jobb.skapadAt.toISOString() };
  })));
});

// POST /api/lego – create new job
router.post("/", async (req, res) => {
  const { kund, datumIn, anteckning, kopplatSpecId, kopplatJobbId, showCertificationOnDocument } = req.body as any;
  const alternate = alternateSenderPatch(req.body?.alternateSender);
  if (alternate.error) return res.status(400).json({ error: alternate.error });
  if (!kund) return res.status(400).json({ error: "kund krävs" });
  const [maxRow] = await db.select({ max: sql<number>`COALESCE(MAX(jobb_nr), 0)::int` }).from(legoJobbTable);
  const jobbNr = (maxRow?.max ?? 0) + 1;
  const [jobb] = await db.insert(legoJobbTable).values({
    jobbNr,
    kund,
    datumIn: datumIn || null,
    anteckning: anteckning || null,
    kopplatSpecId: kopplatSpecId ? Number(kopplatSpecId) : null,
    kopplatJobbId: kopplatJobbId ? Number(kopplatJobbId) : null,
    status: "mottaget",
    showCertificationOnDocument: showCertificationOnDocument === true,
    ...alternate.values,
  }).returning();
  res.json(await mapJobWithSender(jobb));
});

// GET /api/lego/statistik
router.get("/statistik", async (req, res) => {
  const rows = await db
    .select({
      manad: sql<string>`TO_CHAR(${legoJobbTable.skapadAt}, 'YYYY-MM')`,
      kund: legoJobbTable.kund,
      antalJobb: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${legoJobbTable.id})::int`,
      antalPaket: sql<number>`COUNT(${legoPaketTable.id})::int`,
      totalM3: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${legoPaketTable.m3}::numeric), 0)::float`,
      totalPris: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${legoPaketTable.m3}::numeric * ${legoPaketTable.prisM3}::numeric), 0)::float`,
    })
    .from(legoJobbTable)
    .leftJoin(legoPaketTable, eq(legoPaketTable.jobbId, legoJobbTable.id))
    .groupBy(sql`TO_CHAR(${legoJobbTable.skapadAt}, 'YYYY-MM')`, legoJobbTable.kund)
    .orderBy(sql`TO_CHAR(${legoJobbTable.skapadAt}, 'YYYY-MM') DESC`, legoJobbTable.kund);
  res.json(rows);
});

// GET /api/lego/vs-eget – comparison: lego actM3 vs own work actM3, per customer
router.get("/vs-eget", async (req, res) => {
  // Total lego actM3 (use hyvl dims if available, else stored m3)
  const legoRows = await db
    .select({
      kund: legoJobbTable.kund,
      antalJobb: sql<number>`COUNT(DISTINCT ${legoJobbTable.id})::int`,
      actM3: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(
        CASE WHEN ${legoPaketTable.hyvlTjocklek} IS NOT NULL
             AND ${legoPaketTable.hyvlBredd} IS NOT NULL
             AND ${legoPaketTable.langdDm} IS NOT NULL
             AND ${legoPaketTable.antal} IS NOT NULL
          THEN (${legoPaketTable.hyvlTjocklek}::numeric / 1000)
             * (${legoPaketTable.hyvlBredd}::numeric / 1000)
             * (${legoPaketTable.langdDm}::numeric / 10)
             * ${legoPaketTable.antal}::numeric
          ELSE ${legoPaketTable.m3}::numeric
        END
      ), 0)::float`,
    })
    .from(legoJobbTable)
    .leftJoin(legoPaketTable, eq(legoPaketTable.jobbId, legoJobbTable.id))
    .groupBy(legoJobbTable.kund)
    .orderBy(sql`3 DESC`);

  // Total own-work actM3 from inventory (items shipped out)
  const [egetRow] = await db
    .select({
      egetM3: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(
        CASE WHEN ${lagerPosterTable.actM3}::numeric > 0
          THEN ${lagerPosterTable.actM3}::numeric
          ELSE ${lagerPosterTable.m3}::numeric
        END
      ), 0)::float`,
    })
    .from(lagerPosterTable)
    .where(isNotNull(lagerPosterTable.datumUt));

  const legoTotalM3 = legoRows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.actM3, 0);
  const egetM3 = egetRow?.egetM3 ?? 0;
  const totalM3 = legoTotalM3 + egetM3;

  res.json({
    legoM3: +legoTotalM3.toFixed(3),
    egetM3: +egetM3.toFixed(3),
    totalM3: +totalM3.toFixed(3),
    legoAndel: totalM3 > 0 ? +((legoTotalM3 / totalM3) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0,
    egetAndel: totalM3 > 0 ? +((egetM3 / totalM3) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0,
    perKund: legoRows.map(r => ({
      kund: r.kund,
      antalJobb: r.antalJobb,
      actM3: +r.actM3.toFixed(3),
      andel: totalM3 > 0 ? +((r.actM3 / totalM3) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0,
      legoAndel: legoTotalM3 > 0 ? +((r.actM3 / legoTotalM3) * 100).toFixed(1) : 0,
    })),
  });
});

// GET /api/lego/export – Excel export of all jobs
router.get("/export", async (req, res) => {
  const jobs = await db
    .select({
      id: legoJobbTable.id,
      jobbNr: legoJobbTable.jobbNr,
      kund: legoJobbTable.kund,
      datumIn: legoJobbTable.datumIn,
      datumUt: legoJobbTable.datumUt,
      status: legoJobbTable.status,
      anteckning: legoJobbTable.anteckning,
      skapadAt: legoJobbTable.skapadAt,
      antalPaket: sql<number>`COUNT(${legoPaketTable.id})::int`,
      totalM3: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${legoPaketTable.m3}::numeric), 0)::float`,
      totalPris: sql<number>`COALESCE(SUM(${legoPaketTable.m3}::numeric * ${legoPaketTable.prisM3}::numeric), 0)::float`,
    })
    .from(legoJobbTable)
    .leftJoin(legoPaketTable, eq(legoPaketTable.jobbId, legoJobbTable.id))
    .groupBy(legoJobbTable.id)
    .orderBy(desc(legoJobbTable.skapadAt));

  const wsData = [
    ["Jobb #", "Kund", "Mottaget", "Levererat", "Status", "Paket", "m³", "Totalpris (kr)", "Anteckning"],
    ...jobs.map(j => [
      j.jobbNr ?? j.id,
      j.kund,
      j.datumIn ?? "",
      j.datumUt ?? "",
      j.status,
      j.antalPaket,
      n(j.totalM3),
      n(j.totalPris, 2),
      j.anteckning ?? "",
    ]),
  ];

  const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();
  const ws = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(wsData);
  ws["!cols"] = [6, 20, 12, 12, 12, 8, 10, 14, 30].map(w => ({ wch: w }));
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, ws, "Legoarbeten");
  const buf = XLSX.write(wb, { type: "buffer", bookType: "xlsx" });
  res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", `attachment; filename="lego-export.xlsx"`);
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet");
  res.send(buf);
});

// GET /api/lego/:id
router.get("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  const [jobb] = await db.select().from(legoJobbTable).where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, id));
  if (!jobb) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Jobb saknas" });
  const paket = await db.select().from(legoPaketTable).where(eq(legoPaketTable.jobbId, id)).orderBy(legoPaketTable.id);

  let kopplatSpec: { id: number; typ: string; hyvlingNr: number | null; kund: string | null; status: string } | null = null;
  if (jobb.kopplatSpecId) {
    const [s] = await db
      .select({ id: specifikationerTable.id, typ: specifikationerTable.typ, hyvlingNr: specifikationerTable.hyvlingNr, kund: specifikationerTable.kund, status: specifikationerTable.status })
      .from(specifikationerTable)
      .where(eq(specifikationerTable.id, jobb.kopplatSpecId));
    if (s) kopplatSpec = s;
  }

  let kopplatJobb: { id: number; jobbNr: number | null; kund: string; status: string } | null = null;
  if (jobb.kopplatJobbId) {
    const [j] = await db
      .select({ id: legoJobbTable.id, jobbNr: legoJobbTable.jobbNr, kund: legoJobbTable.kund, status: legoJobbTable.status })
      .from(legoJobbTable)
      .where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, jobb.kopplatJobbId));
    if (j) kopplatJobb = j;
  }

  res.json({
    ...(await mapJobWithSender(jobb)),
    skapadAt: jobb.skapadAt.toISOString(),
    uppdateradAt: jobb.uppdateradAt.toISOString(),
    kopplatSpec,
    kopplatJobb,
    paket: paket.map(mapPaket),
  });
});

// GET /api/lego/:id/export – Excel export of single job
router.get("/:id/export", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  const [jobb] = await db.select().from(legoJobbTable).where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, id));
  if (!jobb) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Jobb saknas" });
  const paket = await db.select().from(legoPaketTable).where(eq(legoPaketTable.jobbId, id)).orderBy(legoPaketTable.id);

  const mapped = paket.map(p => ({
    pktNr: p.pktNr ?? "",
    traslag: p.traslag ?? "",
    kvalitet: p.kvalitet ?? "",
    rawTjocklek: toNum(p.rawTjocklek),
    rawBredd: toNum(p.rawBredd),
    hyvlatTill: p.hyvlatTill ?? "",
    hyvlTjocklek: toNum(p.hyvlTjocklek),
    hyvlBredd: toNum(p.hyvlBredd),
    langdDm: toNum(p.langdDm),
    antal: p.antal,
    lpm: toNum(p.lpm),
    m3: toNum(p.m3),
    prisM3: toNum(p.prisM3),
    totalpris: toNum(p.m3) != null && toNum(p.prisM3) != null
      ? parseFloat((toNum(p.m3)! * toNum(p.prisM3)!).toFixed(2)) : null,
    anteckning: p.anteckning ?? "",
  }));

  const totalM3 = mapped.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.m3 ?? 0), 0);
  const totalPris = mapped.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.totalpris ?? 0), 0);
  const totalAntal = mapped.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.antal ?? 0), 0);
  const totalLpm = mapped.reduce((s, p) => s + (p.lpm ?? 0), 0);

  const wb = XLSX.utils.book_new();

  // Sheet 1: Job info
  const infoData = [
    ["Jobb #", jobb.jobbNr ?? jobb.id],
    ["Kund", jobb.kund],
    ["Mottaget", jobb.datumIn ?? ""],
    ["Levererat", jobb.datumUt ?? ""],
    ["Status", jobb.status],
    ["Anteckning", jobb.anteckning ?? ""],
    [],
    ["Totalt antal", totalAntal],
    ["Total Lpm", n(totalLpm, 1)],
    ["Total m³", n(totalM3)],
    ["Totalpris (kr)", n(totalPris, 2)],
  ];
  const wsInfo = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(infoData);
  wsInfo["!cols"] = [{ wch: 16 }, { wch: 30 }];
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsInfo, "Jobböversikt");

  // Sheet 2: Packages
  const pkgData = [
    ["Pkt nr", "Träslag", "Kval", "Råv T (mm)", "Råv B (mm)", "Hyvlat till", "Utdim T", "Utdim B", "Längd (dm)", "Antal", "Lpm", "m³", "Pris/m³", "Totalpris (kr)", "Anteckning"],
    ...mapped.map(p => [
      p.pktNr, p.traslag, p.kvalitet,
      p.rawTjocklek, p.rawBredd,
      p.hyvlatTill, p.hyvlTjocklek, p.hyvlBredd,
      p.langdDm, p.antal, p.lpm, p.m3,
      p.prisM3, p.totalpris,
      p.anteckning,
    ]),
    [],
    ["", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", "", totalAntal, n(totalLpm, 1), n(totalM3), "", n(totalPris, 2), ""],
  ];
  const wsPkt = XLSX.utils.aoa_to_sheet(pkgData);
  wsPkt["!cols"] = [8, 10, 8, 10, 10, 12, 10, 10, 12, 8, 10, 10, 10, 14, 20].map(w => ({ wch: w }));
  XLSX.utils.book_append_sheet(wb, wsPkt, "Paket");

  const buf = XLSX.write(wb, { type: "buffer", bookType: "xlsx" });
  const filename = `lego-${jobb.jobbNr ?? jobb.id}-${jobb.kund.replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, "_")}.xlsx`;
  res.setHeader("Content-Disposition", `attachment; filename="${filename}"`);
  res.setHeader("Content-Type", "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet");
  res.send(buf);
});

// PUT /api/lego/:id
router.put("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  const { kund, datumIn, datumUt, anteckning, status, paket, kopplatSpecId, kopplatJobbId, showCertificationOnDocument } = req.body as any;
  const alternate = alternateSenderPatch(req.body?.alternateSender);
  if (alternate.error) return res.status(400).json({ error: alternate.error });
  await db.update(legoJobbTable).set({
    kund: kund ?? undefined,
    datumIn: datumIn ?? null,
    datumUt: datumUt ?? null,
    anteckning: anteckning ?? null,
    status: status ?? undefined,
    showCertificationOnDocument: typeof showCertificationOnDocument === "boolean" ? showCertificationOnDocument : undefined,
    kopplatSpecId: kopplatSpecId !== undefined ? (kopplatSpecId ? Number(kopplatSpecId) : null) : undefined,
    kopplatJobbId: kopplatJobbId !== undefined ? (kopplatJobbId ? Number(kopplatJobbId) : null) : undefined,
    ...alternate.values,
    uppdateradAt: new Date(),
  }).where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, id));

  if (Array.isArray(paket)) {
    await db.delete(legoPaketTable).where(eq(legoPaketTable.jobbId, id));
    if (paket.length > 0) {
      await db.insert(legoPaketTable).values(paket.map((p: any) => ({
        jobbId: id,
        pktNr: p.pktNr || null,
        traslag: p.traslag || null,
        kvalitet: p.kvalitet || null,
        rawTjocklek: p.rawTjocklek?.toString() || null,
        rawBredd: p.rawBredd?.toString() || null,
        hyvlatTill: p.hyvlatTill || null,
        hyvlTjocklek: p.hyvlTjocklek?.toString() || null,
        hyvlBredd: p.hyvlBredd?.toString() || null,
        langdDm: p.langdDm?.toString() || null,
        antal: p.antal ? parseInt(p.antal) : null,
        m3: p.m3?.toString() || null,
        lpm: p.lpm?.toString() || null,
        prisM3: p.prisM3?.toString() || null,
        anteckning: p.anteckning || null,
      })));
    }
  }
  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// POST /api/lego/:id/leverera – mark as levererat
router.post("/:id/leverera", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  const [job] = await db.select().from(legoJobbTable).where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, id));
  if (!job) return res.status(404).json({ error: "Jobb saknas" });
  if (!hasSenderSnapshot(job)) {
    const [branding] = await db.select().from(brandingSettingsTable).where(eq(brandingSettingsTable.id, 1)).limit(1);
    const source = branding
      ? documentSenderSource(alternateSenderProfile(job), brandingToDocumentProfile(branding))
      : null;
    const missing = source ? missingDocumentProfileFields(source) : ["företagsprofil"];
    if (missing.length > 0) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        error: `Legoordern kan inte levereras eftersom dokumentavsändaren är ofullständig. Fyll i ${missing.join(", ")}.`,
      });
    }
  }
  await db.update(legoJobbTable).set({
    status: "levererat",
    datumUt: today,
    uppdateradAt: new Date(),
  }).where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, id));
  await captureLegoSenderSnapshot(job);
  const [updated] = await db.select().from(legoJobbTable).where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, id));
  res.json({ ok: true, jobb: await mapJobWithSender(updated) });
});

// POST /api/lego/:id/ateroppen
router.post("/:id/ateroppen", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  await db.update(legoJobbTable).set({
    status: "hyvlat",
    datumUt: null,
    uppdateradAt: new Date(),
  }).where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, id));
  res.json({ ok: true });
});

// DELETE /api/lego/:id
router.delete("/:id", async (req, res) => {
  const id = parseInt(req.params.id);
  await db.delete(legoJobbTable).where(eq(legoJobbTable.id, id));
  res.json({ ok: true });
});

export default router;


================ FILE: artifacts/api-server/src/routes/omraden.ts ================

import { Router } from "express";
import { db, lagerOmradenTable, lagerFackTable, lagerPosterTable } from "@workspace/db";
import { and, eq, isNull, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { iLagerConditions } from "../lib/lagerFilters";

const router = Router();

// Samma volymfält (m3, inte actM3) som statistiken i routes/lager.ts (t.ex.
// LagerStatistik och per-plats-statistiken) redan använder, för att
// områdes-/fackstatistik alltid ska matcha den befintliga Lagerplatser-vyn.
function m3Sql() {
  return sql<string>`coalesce(round(sum(coalesce(${lagerPosterTable.m3}::numeric, 0)), 3), 0)::float`;
}

router.get("/", async (_req, res) => {
  const omraden = await db.select().from(lagerOmradenTable).where(eq(lagerOmradenTable.aktiv, true));

  const [statRows, fackCountRows] = await Promise.all([
    db
      .select({
        omradeId: lagerPosterTable.omradeId,
        totalM3: m3Sql(),
        posterAntal: sql<number>`count(*)`,
        totalAntal: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${lagerPosterTable.antal}), 0)`,
      })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(and(sql`${lagerPosterTable.omradeId} is not null`, ...iLagerConditions()))
      .groupBy(lagerPosterTable.omradeId),
    db
      .select({ omradeId: lagerFackTable.omradeId, fackAntal: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(lagerFackTable)
      .where(eq(lagerFackTable.aktiv, true))
      .groupBy(lagerFackTable.omradeId),
  ]);

  const statsByOmrade = new Map(statRows.map((r) => [r.omradeId, r]));
  const fackCountByOmrade = new Map(fackCountRows.map((r) => [r.omradeId, Number(r.fackAntal)]));

  res.json(
    omraden.map((o) => {
      const stat = statsByOmrade.get(o.id);
      return {
        id: o.id,
        namn: o.namn,
        slug: o.slug,
        mapEnabled: o.mapEnabled,
        totalM3: Number(stat?.totalM3 ?? 0),
        posterAntal: Number(stat?.posterAntal ?? 0),
        totalAntal: Number(stat?.totalAntal ?? 0),
        fackAntal: fackCountByOmrade.get(o.id) ?? 0,
      };
    }),
  );
});

router.get("/:slug", async (req, res) => {
  const { slug } = req.params;
  const [omrade] = await db.select().from(lagerOmradenTable).where(eq(lagerOmradenTable.slug, slug));
  if (!omrade) {
    res.status(404).json({ error: "Område hittades inte" });
    return;
  }

  const [fackRows, fackStatRows, ejAngivetRows] = await Promise.all([
    db
      .select()
      .from(lagerFackTable)
      .where(and(eq(lagerFackTable.omradeId, omrade.id), eq(lagerFackTable.aktiv, true)))
      .orderBy(lagerFackTable.sortering),
    db
      .select({
        fackId: lagerPosterTable.fackId,
        totalM3: m3Sql(),
        posterAntal: sql<number>`count(*)`,
        totalAntal: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${lagerPosterTable.antal}), 0)`,
      })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(and(eq(lagerPosterTable.omradeId, omrade.id), sql`${lagerPosterTable.fackId} is not null`, ...iLagerConditions()))
      .groupBy(lagerPosterTable.fackId),
    db
      .select({
        totalM3: m3Sql(),
        posterAntal: sql<number>`count(*)`,
        totalAntal: sql<number>`coalesce(sum(${lagerPosterTable.antal}), 0)`,
      })
      .from(lagerPosterTable)
      .where(and(eq(lagerPosterTable.omradeId, omrade.id), isNull(lagerPosterTable.fackId), ...iLagerConditions())),
  ]);

  const statByFack = new Map(fackStatRows.map((r) => [r.fackId, r]));
  const fack = fackRows.map((f) => {
    const stat = statByFack.get(f.id);
    return {
      id: f.id,
      kod: f.kod,
      sortering: f.sortering,
      totalM3: Number(stat?.totalM3 ?? 0),
      posterAntal: Number(stat?.posterAntal ?? 0),
      totalAntal: Number(stat?.totalAntal ?? 0),
    };
  });

  const totalM3 = fack.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.totalM3, 0) + Number(ejAngivetRows[0]?.totalM3 ?? 0);
  const posterAntal = fack.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.posterAntal, 0) + Number(ejAngivetRows[0]?.posterAntal ?? 0);
  const totalAntal = fack.reduce((sum, f) => sum + f.totalAntal, 0) + Number(ejAngivetRows[0]?.totalAntal ?? 0);

  res.json({
    id: omrade.id,
    namn: omrade.namn,
    slug: omrade.slug,
    mapEnabled: omrade.mapEnabled,
    totalM3,
    posterAntal,
    totalAntal,
    fackAntal: fack.length,
    fack,
    ejAngivet: {
      totalM3: Number(ejAngivetRows[0]?.totalM3 ?? 0),
      posterAntal: Number(ejAngivetRows[0]?.posterAntal ?? 0),
      totalAntal: Number(ejAngivetRows[0]?.totalAntal ?? 0),
    },
  });
});

export default router;
